### [1] [Improving Jet A-1 Thermal-Oxidative Stability through Selective Removal of Unwanted Trace Species via 3.7 Å Chabazite Filtration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03375)
*Morteza Roostaeinia,Ehsan Alborzi,Xue Yong,Kyungwha Park,Vsevolod Ivanov*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过DFT和AIMD计算，系统研究了3.7 Å菱沸石组成（Si/Al比和双金属掺杂）对Jet A-1燃料中热氧化沉积促进剂去除与抗氧化剂丁基羟基甲苯保留之间权衡的影响，建立了吸附-输运-电子筛选的统一框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Jet A-1燃料的热稳定性受微量杂原子物种（促进热氧化沉积形成）和抗氧化添加剂（如BHT）的共同影响。3.7 Å菱沸石可去除沉积促进剂，但需平衡促进剂去除与抗氧化剂损失之间的权衡，优化其组成成为关键挑战。

Method: 采用密度泛函理论（DFT）和从头算分子动力学（AIMD）模拟，以框架酸性（Si/Al = 35–8）和双金属取代（Co, Mg, Zn，固定Al含量）为组成描述符，评估吸附能、分子迁移率及电子结构特征。

Result: 降低Si/Al比增强所有物种（包括BHT）的吸附，存在固有的选择性惩罚；双金属取代引入化学选择性行为，可增强特定促进剂的吸附同时抑制或增强BHT吸附；AIMD显示400 K下吸附能不足以描述趋势，分子迁移率影响停留时间和实际捕获效率；态密度和波函数分析揭示了与掺杂相关的吸附偏移特征。

Conclusion: 建立了统一的吸附-输运-电子筛选框架，用于选择既能去除沉积促进剂又能保留Jet A-1中抗氧化剂功能的菱沸石组成。

Abstract: The thermal stability of Jet A-1 fuel is strongly affected by trace heteroatomic species that promote thermal oxidative deposit formation, as well as antioxidant additives such as butylated hydroxytoluene, which preserve fuel stability. 3.7 Å chabazite is a tunable microporous adsorbent, but optimizing its composition requires balancing promoter removal against antioxidant loss. Here, we use density functional theory and \textit{ab initio} molecular dynamics (AIMD) to evaluate this trade-off using two compositional descriptors: framework acidity (Si/Al $= 35$--$8$) and bimetallic substitution (Co, Mg, Zn) at fixed Al content. Lowering Si/Al strengthens adsorption of all species, including BHT, confirming an intrinsic selectivity penalty for acidity-only tuning. In contrast, bimetallic substitution introduces chemically selective behavior, strengthening uptake of specific promoters while either suppressing or enhancing antioxidant adsorption depending on dopant identity. AIMD simulations at 400 K further show that adsorption energy alone is insufficient to describe calculated trends, because molecular mobility inside chabazite can influence residence time and effective capture during fuel treatment. Analysis of the density of states and the participating wavefunctions reveals signatures that are consistent with dopant-dependent adsorption shifts. These results establish a unified adsorption--transport--electronic screening framework for selecting chabazite compositions that remove deposit promoters while preserving antioxidant functionality in Jet A-1 treatment.

</details>


### [2] [AquaGen: Scaling generative models to molecular dynamics precision on thousands of atoms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03513)
*Emmanuel Bengio,Sanjeev Raja,Yui Tik Pang,Kerstin Klaeser,Cristian Gabellini,Nikhil Shenoy,Francesco Di Giovanni,Prudencio Tossou*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: AquaGen是一个全原子、显式溶剂、周期性边界条件感知的生成模型，能够以分子动力学几分之一的成本从玻尔兹曼分布生成分子构型，并在绝对水化自由能估计上实现4-10倍加速且精度相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成模型通常操作在粗粒化、真空或隐式溶剂系统上，去除了大量自由度，导致无法进行力场能量评估和MD模拟后处理；作者希望构建一个高分辨率（全原子显式溶剂）的生成模型，填补这一空白。

Method: 提出AquaGen，一个全原子、显式溶剂、考虑周期性边界条件的生成模型，直接从玻尔兹曼分布生成分子构型；生成的样本可进行力场能量后处理和MD模拟，通过系综平均以灰盒方式预测性质；利用来自非平衡玻尔兹曼分布的不相关样本进行自由能估计。

Result: 在绝对水化自由能估计任务上，AquaGen比标准GPU-based MD快4-10倍，且精度相当；通过增大模型规模和生成更多样本，可获得可预测的性能提升；生成不相关样本的能力使系综预测更准确、可解释且可优化，并带有校准的不确定性估计。

Conclusion: 高分辨率系综生成方法在自由能估计中展示了实用价值，未来有望替代分子动力学在亲脂性、膜通透性或绝对结合自由能预测等任务中的应用，对药物和材料开发具有重要意义。

Abstract: We present AquaGen, the first all-atom, explicit solvent, periodic-boundary-condition-aware generative model that produces molecular configurations from the Boltzmann distribution at a fraction of the cost of molecular dynamics (MD). This is in contrast with existing generative models that remove degrees of freedom by operating on coarse-grained, vacuum, or implicit solvent systems. Operating at this resolution allows for post-processing through force field energy evaluations and MD simulations, and enables the prediction of relevant properties in a gray-box manner (as ensemble averages of potential energy evaluations over generated samples). We demonstrate the utility of this paradigm on absolute hydration free energy (AHFE), producing estimates 4-10x faster and with comparable accuracy to standard GPU-based MD. By generating uncorrelated samples from alchemical Boltzmann distributions, we create more accurate, interpretable, and refinable ensemble predictions with calibrated uncertainty estimates, unlike regression methods which are entirely black-box predictors. Our approach also yields predictable benefits from increasing train- and test-time compute, realized by scaling model size and generating more samples, respectively. We believe that this approach demonstrates the utility of high-resolution ensemble generation for free energy estimation, with future potential to replace MD in tasks such as the prediction of lipophilicity, membrane permeability, or absolute binding free energy (ABFE) -- whose grounding and interpretability may be critical for the development of new drugs and materials.

</details>


### [3] [Application of SAP-X2C to Spectroscopy and Comparison to Screened Nuclear Spin--Orbit Approximations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03814)
*Yannick J. Franzke,Christof Holzer*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 本文推广了SAP-X2C方法到解析导数理论并应用于分子光谱计算，通过与四组分结果和SNSO-X2C对比，证明SAP-X2C在NMR、EPR、Mössbauer、UV/vis及X射线吸收光谱中表现优异，因其具有明确的热化学极限和较少的经验性，有望成为DFT光谱计算中修正双电子图像变化误差的默认方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Surjuse和Valeev提出了SAP-X2C方法用于修正单电子X2C理论中的双电子图像变化误差，但尚未将其推广到解析导数理论和分子光谱计算中，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 将SAP-X2C（原子势叠加）方法推广到解析导数理论，并在NMR、EPR、Mössbauer、UV/vis及X射线吸收光谱等多种光谱性质中进行系统测试，与四组分基准结果和SNSO-X2C方法进行对比评估。

Result: SNSO-X2C和SAP-X2C在光谱性质计算中均表现优异；SAP-X2C的主要优势在于其具有明确的热化学极限和较少的经验性。

Conclusion: SAP-X2C因其准确性、简洁性和高效性，有望成为DFT光谱计算中修正双电子图像变化误差的默认方法；基于原子平均场方法的更复杂方案仍适用于高精度相关方法和热化学计算。

Abstract: Recently, Surjuse and Valeev [\textit{J. Chem. Theory Comput.} \textbf{22}, 3443--3452 (2026).] suggested to use a simple superposition of atomic potentials to account for two-electron picture-change error in one-electron exact two-component (SAP-X2C) theory. Herein, we generalize this ansatz to analytical derivative theory and apply SAP-X2C to molecular spectroscopy. The accuracy is assessed for NMR, EPR, Mössbauer, UV/vis, as well as X-ray absorption spectroscopy. A thorough comparison with four-component results and the even simpler screened nuclear spin--orbit (SNSO) approximation reveals that both SNSO-X2C and SAP-X2C perform excellently for spectroscopic properties. The major advantage of SAP-X2C over SNSO-X2C is consequently its well defined thermochemical limit and its less empirical nature. Therefore, SAP-X2C may be expected to become the default choice to mitigate the two-electron picture-change error in density functional theory approaches for spectroscopy thanks to its accuracy, simplicity, and efficiency. More complicated approaches to account for this error based on atomic mean-field ansätze may still be relevant for high-level correlated methods and highly accurate thermochemistry.

</details>


### [4] [ML and AI for density functional theory: different priorities for Kohn-Sham and orbital-free DFT, for electronic and nuclear DFT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04095)
*Xin-Hui Wu,Sergei Manzhos*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 本文综述了机器学习（ML）和人工智能（AI）技术在电子DFT和核DFT中的计算瓶颈与精度需求的异同，重点讨论了不同DFT任务（交换关联泛函、动能泛函、密度或基函数）适用的ML方法选择。指出深度神经网络在KS-DFT中有效，但在构建动能泛函时存在劣势，而符号回归在电子和核DFT中均具有概念优势但尚未充分实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 系统梳理ML/AI在电子DFT和核DFT中的应用现状，识别两类DFT在计算瓶颈和精度需求上的异同，为方法迁移和算法选择提供指导。

Method: 通过对比分析电子DFT和核DFT的特点，分别讨论ML学习交换关联泛函、动能泛函、密度或基函数等不同任务，评估深度神经网络与符号回归等方法的适用性。

Result: 深度神经网络在KS-DFT（学习交换关联泛函）中表现优异，但在构建动能泛函时存在劣势；符号回归在两类DFT中均显示出概念优势但尚未完全实现。

Conclusion: ML-enhanced电子DFT中的一些有前景的方法可以迁移到核DFT中，但需要根据具体任务选择合适的ML技术。

Abstract: We overview similarities and, importantly, differences in computational bottlenecks and accuracy requirements that can be addressed with machine learning (ML) and artificial intelligence (AI) techniques in electronic and nuclear DFT. From these follow different promising methodological and algorithmic choices depending on whether one machine learns the exchange correlation (XC) functional, the kinetic energy functional (KEF), the density or the basis functions. In particular, while the popular deep neural networks remain a potent choice in the context of KS DFT, we highlight their disadvantages when building KEFs and highlight conceptual advantages - yet to be fully realized - of symbolic regression for both electronic and nuclear DFT. We point out promising approaches that can be carried from the more extensively investigated ML-enhanced electronic DFT to nuclear DFT.

</details>


### [5] [Differentiable OPLS Force Field Parameterization for Ionic Electrolytes and High-Throughput Application to Lithium-ion Batteries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04633)
*Haichao Huang,Zilin Chen,Qi Liu,Tianqi Zhao,Yunpei Liu,Guotao Qiu,Jianhui Chen,Zhen Li,Wenshuo Liang,Minsung Cho,Manxue Zhang,Feiyu Kang,Xiaolong Zou,Yidan Cao,Xushan Zhao,Ziqi Cheng,Ye Mei*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种自动化的可微分OPLS-AA力场参数化流程，通过高通量MD模拟生成超10,000种电解液配方的五类核心性质数据集，为数据驱动的锂离子电解液理性设计奠定基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离子电解液的设计受限于溶剂-盐组合空间巨大和传统试错法效率低下，经典力场对多组分体系精度不足。

Method: 开发自动化可微分OPLS-AA力场参数化流程，采用拓扑引导的原子类型化减少参数冗余，通过DMFF框架优化Lennard-Jones参数，以实验密度为拟合目标、离子电导率为独立验证指标。在天琼平台上对67种溶剂和15种锂盐的超10,000种配方进行了高通量MD模拟。

Result: 生成了涵盖密度、介电常数、粘度、扩散系数和离子电导率五类核心性质的综合数据集。t-SNE可视化揭示了不同盐化学的部分聚类、浓度和温度下的连续性质梯度，以及内部物理自洽性，溶剂组分被识别为另一个关键性能调节因子。

Conclusion: 准确的传递性力场和大规模数据集为数据驱动的离子电解液理性设计提供了坚实基础。

Abstract: The rational design of ionic electrolytes for lithium-ion batteries (LIBs) is severely constrained by the vast solvent-salt combinatorial space and low efficiency of empirical trial-and-error. While molecular dynamics (MD) bridges microscopic solvation structures and macroscopic physicochemical properties, classical force fields often lack sufficient accuracy for multicomponent systems. To address these challenges, we develop an automated differentiable OPLS-AA force field parameterization workflow tailored for general ionic electrolytes. It employs topology-guided atom typification to reduce parameter redundancy and optimizes Lennard-Jones parameters via the DMFF framework, with experimental density as the fitting target and ionic conductivity as an independent validation metric. Rigorous convergence tests yield a standardized simulation protocol with $\sim$100,000-atom systems and 35-40 ns NVT runs to ensure reliable transport property quantification. High-throughput MD simulations of over 10,000 formulations spanning 67 solvents and 15 lithium salts are conducted on the Tianqiong platform, generating a comprehensive dataset covering five core properties: density, dielectric constant, viscosity, diffusion coefficient, and ionic conductivity. t-SNE visualization reveals partial clustering of distinct salt chemistries, continuous property gradients with concentration and temperature, and internal physical self-consistency, with solvent composition identified as another key performance regulator. Together, the accurate transferable force field and large-scale dataset provide a solid foundation for data-driven rational design of ionic electrolytes.

</details>


### [6] [Physically-Relevant Information Learning in High-Dimensional Time-Derivatives Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05127)
*Domiziano Doria,Matteo Becchi,Giovanni M. Pavan*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出时间导数（TiDe）空间方法，通过构建高维时间导数空间，无需降维即可从时序数据中提取结构和动力学信息，实现对复杂多体动力系统的有效分析，并在分子动力学模拟和实验追踪数据中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法仅关注相对位置、对称性等序参量，难以完整捕捉复杂动力系统的物理知识。为更全面地理解系统，需要同时关联结构信息与动力学信息，为此提出一种高效构建和导航高维时间导数空间的方法。

Method: 从观测时间序列数据中构建TiDe空间，每个维度对应不同阶次的时间导数，包含不同类型物理现象/事件的信息。通过无监督方法直接从TiDe空间中提取信息，无需先验降维。

Result: 在分子动力学模拟和实验追踪两类复杂动力系统数据集上的实验表明，TiDe空间能够高效导航和学习，提供直观可解释的分析结果。

Conclusion: TiDe空间为从观测时序数据中研究复杂动力系统提供了一个鲁棒的通用分析框架，能够有效同时捕捉结构和动力学信息。

Abstract: Understanding the physics of many-body complex dynamical systems is typically non-trivial. High-dimensional analysis approaches are often deemed necessary to prevent losing important information. Typically, these use order parameters or descriptors capturing information related to, e.g., relative positions, symmetries, etc., of the units in the studied system. However, in many cases, gaining information related to the relative positions (or velocities) of the constitutive units alone may be insufficient, and to reach a more complete physical knowledge, one should ideally learn and correlate with each other both structure and dynamics. Here we demonstrate how to efficiently achieve such a goal by building and navigating high-dimensional Time-Derivatives (TiDe) space. A TiDe space can be easily generated for virtually any type of system/phenomenon under study from the time-series data collected along its observation over time. Each TiDe's dimension corresponds to a growing-order time-derivative of the extracted data, thus containing information related to different types of physical phenomena/events that can be easily extracted via unsupervised approaches. We demonstrate how, by definition, TiDes can be directly analyzed without a need for prior dimensionality reduction, providing results that are intrinsically intuitive to interpret. We show the potential of the method by analyzing two prototypical example datasets extracted from molecular dynamics simulations or experimental tracking of different complex dynamical systems. Our results demonstrate how efficiently one can navigate and learn in such information-rich TiDe spaces, which provide robust general frameworks for data analysis and for studying complex dynamical systems from the data collected along their observation over time.

</details>

### [7] [QuantFlow: A Federated Mamba-Based Post-Transformer Foundation Model for Time-Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02632)
*Shah Nawaz Haider,Steve Austin,Arnab Barua,Sarowar Morshed Shawon,Hadaate Ullah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: QuantFlow结合倒置序列嵌入、双向Mamba状态空间解码器、分位数回归和联邦学习，实现了可扩展、不确定性感知且保护隐私的时间序列概率预测，在加密货币、交通、电力等多个领域取得良好效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列基础模型依赖集中式数据和Transformer注意力机制，难以处理长序列、高维度和隐私敏感的信号，亟需一种既能跨任务迁移又兼顾隐私保护的预测框架。

Method: 每个变量在完整观测窗口上嵌入，通过双向Mamba状态空间解码器进行前向和反向处理，投影到五个条件分位数；结合TSMixup通过Dirichlet加权插值增强时间多样性，并在联邦学习框架下实现非集中式训练。

Result: 在ETTm1上MSE为0.2834，Weather上为0.2218；20客户端非独立同分布联邦部署在三轮通信后仍保持有效精度，无需集中原始数据。

Conclusion: 选择性状态空间建模是可扩展、不确定性感知和隐私保护的时间序列预测的有前景的基础，但在不规则流行病信号和长时序泛化上存在局限。

Abstract: Time-series forecasting supports decisions in finance, en-ergy, transportation, public health, and industrial monitoring. Recent foundation models improve transfer across forecast-ing tasks, but many depend on centralized data and Trans-former attention, which restricts their use for long, high-di-mensional, and privacy-sensitive signals. This paper presents QuantFlow, a probabilistic forecasting framework that com-bines inverted sequence embedding, bidirectional Mamba state-space decoders, quantile regression, and federated learning. Each variable is embedded over the complete ob-servation window, processed in forward and reverse direc-tions, and projected to five conditional quantiles. TSMixup expands temporal diversity through Dirichlet-weighted inter-polation while preserving sequence structure. Experiments cover cryptocurrency, traffic, electricity, Electricity Trans-former Temperature, influenza, and weather data. QuantFlow obtains mean squared errors of 0.2834 on ETTm1 and 0.2218 on Weather, and a 20-client non-IID deployment retains use-ful accuracy after three communication rounds without cen-tralizing raw records. The results indicate that selective state-space modelling is a promising basis for scalable, uncer-tainty-aware, and privacy-conscious time-series prediction, while also revealing limitations on irregular epidemiological signals and long-horizon generalization.

</details>


### [8] [Federated Learning for Object Detection: Enabling Collaborative Drone Learning Without Centralizing Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02636)
*Daniel M. Jimenez-Gutierrez,Enrique Zuazua,Georgios Kellaris,Joaquin del Rio,Oleksii Sliusarenko,Xabi Uribe-Etxebarria*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将联邦学习(FL)应用于无人机目标检测任务，使得分布式无人机群可以在不集中图像数据的情况下协作训练共享模型。在KIIT-MiTA数据集上的实验表明，联邦学习方法性能接近集中式训练，显著优于单无人机训练，轻量级模型YOLO26 nano在mAP@0.50和mAP@0.50:0.95上分别获得52.89%和67.80%的相对提升，实现了可扩展、高性能且隐私保护的分布式目标检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全关键的无人机和边缘视觉系统（如灾难响应、安防监控、基础设施检测等）需要高性能目标检测能力，而模型性能依赖大规模持续更新的数据集。然而，传统集中式训练需要汇集航拍图像数据，面临隐私、法规、存储和带宽等挑战，在分布式无人机部署场景中尤为突出，因为机载产生的视觉数据难以或不适合传输到中心化基础设施。

Method: 采用联邦学习(FL)框架，基于Sherpa.ai FL平台在KIIT-MiTA数据集上实现联邦目标检测流水线，与单无人机训练和集中式训练基线进行对比，使用平均精度(mAP)在IoU阈值0.50和0.50-0.95下评估性能。

Result: 联邦学习方法的性能接近集中式训练，同时大幅优于单无人机训练。最佳轻量级模型YOLO26 nano（适用于资源受限的边缘设备）在mAP@0.50和mAP@0.50:0.95上分别取得52.89%和67.80%的相对提升。

Conclusion: 联邦学习能够在无需数据集中化的前提下，实现跨分布式无人机群的可扩展、高性能且保护隐私的目标检测。

Abstract: Object detection is a fundamental capability for AI-driven perception in safety-critical drone and edge-vision systems, including disaster response, operational security environments, infrastructure monitoring and defense applications. Robust model performance in such environments depends on large, continuously updated datasets. However, training high-performing detectors typically requires centralizing aerial imagery, which raises privacy, regulatory, storage, and bandwidth challenges. This is especially problematic in distributed drone deployments, where visual data is generated onboard and is often impractical or undesirable to transfer to a centralized infrastructure.
  In this work, we apply Federated Learning (FL) for object detection, enabling drones to improve a shared model while keeping image data local and private. We implement a federated object detection pipeline using the Sherpa.ai FL platform on the KIIT-MiTA dataset, and compare it with Single-drone and Centralized baselines using mean Average Precision (mAP) at IoU thresholds of 0.50 and 0.50-0.95. In our experiments, the proposed FL approach remains close to Centralized training while dramatically improving over Single-drone training, with the best lightweight model (YOLO26 nano), suitable for deployment even on very limited edge infrastructure, achieving relative gains of 52.89% and 67.80% in mAP@0.50 and mAP@0.50:0.95, respectively. These results show that FL enables scalable, high-performing, and privacy-preserving object detection across distributed drone fleets without data centralization.

</details>


### [9] [Weighted Conformal Prediction for Lab-to-Track Thermal Transfer in EV Motorsport Powertrains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02722)
*Varshith Roy Kotla*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了使用共形预测（Conformal Prediction）方法解决电动汽车动力总成热预测中“实验室到赛道”的迁移问题。在CALCE锂电池数据集上校准了EnbPI方法，在分布内达到95%覆盖率，但在真实协变量偏移（US06工况，45°C）下降至70.13%。引入加权EnbPI（密度比加权）后仅提升至72.42%。在F1 2023遥测数据（Monza和Silverstone赛道）上进行无监督OOD诊断，异常标记率远高于基线。结论：共形域自适应是一个有前景但尚未完全解决的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高性能电动汽车动力总成的热波动难以预测，因为内部温度在实验室外几乎不可观测，且基于实验室循环标定的模型在部署到真实负载时失效。作者希望研究这种“实验室到赛道”的迁移问题，通过共形预测实现分布自由的不确定性量化。

Method: 采用EnbPI（Xu & Xie, 2021）——一种基于留一法bootstrap集成的共形预测方法，适用于自相关时间序列。在CALCE锂电池数据（A123 SP20电芯，FUDS剖面）上标定。引入加权EnbPI，结合EnbPI的集成残差与密度比加权（Tibshirani et al., 2019），通过概率域分类器估计密度比。在F1遥测数据上进行无监督OOD诊断。

Result: 未加权EnbPI在分布内95%名义覆盖率下达到95.00%经验覆盖率；在真实协变量偏移（US06 45°C）下降至70.13%。加权EnbPI提升至72.42%。F1无监督OOD诊断：Monza异常标记率65.6%，Silverstone 58.0%，远高于分布内基准5%。异常标记与制动/DRS区域的关联不一致。

Conclusion: 共形域自适应是解决该问题的一个有前景但仅部分有效的工具，文章详细说明了其不足之处。加权方法带来了诚实的、微小的改进，但并非完全修复方案。

Abstract: Predicting thermal volatility in high-performance EV powertrains is difficult as internal temperatures are rarely observable outside the lab, and models calibrated on lab drive cycles fail when deployed against real-world loads. We study this lab-to-track transfer problem using conformal prediction, offering distribution-free uncertainty bounds. We implement Ensemble Batch Prediction Intervals (EnbPI; Xu & Xie, 2021), a leave-one-out bootstrap-ensemble conformal method for autocorrelated time series, and calibrate it on real CALCE lithium-ion cycler data (A123 SP20 cells, FUDS profile). We evaluate it under a genuine, measured covariate shift: a second real CALCE test condition (US06 Highway Driving Schedule at 45°C). The unweighted EnbPI bound, achieving its nominal 95% coverage in-distribution (measured: 95.00%), degrades to 70.13% empirical coverage under this real shift. We introduce a weighted EnbPI procedure combining EnbPI's ensemble residuals with density-ratio weighting (Tibshirani et al., 2019), estimating the density ratio via a probabilistic domain classifier. This recovers coverage to 72.42%, a modest, honestly-reported improvement, not a complete fix. We additionally apply the calibrated model to real 2023 Formula 1 telemetry (Monza and Silverstone, driver VER) as an unsupervised out-of-distribution diagnostic. Because no internal thermal channel exists in public trackside telemetry, we report only unsupervised flag rates (65.6% at Monza, 58.0% at Silverstone, well above the 5% in-distribution base rate) and note inconsistent associations between flags and braking/DRS zones. We conclude that conformal domain adaptation is a promising but only partially solved tool for this problem, detailing exactly where it falls short.

</details>


### [10] [A Precedent-Guided Co-Scientist for Side-Effect-Aware Drug Redesign](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02944)
*Yujin Kim,Charmgil Hong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PRECEDE是一个基于先例引导的AI协同科学家系统，用于药物重新设计，能够在保留治疗效果的同时减轻指定副作用，通过LLM协调器整合药物-副作用关联、生物医学知识图谱和安全优化先例，并包含人类审查检查点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分子生成方法往往是孤立的，缺乏对药物-副作用关联证据的利用和基于先验药理学知识的约束，难以在保证疗效的前提下有针对性地减轻特定副作用。

Method: PRECEDE将药物重新设计框架为基于证据的推理过程，通过LLM协调器整合三类知识（药物-副作用关联、生物医学知识图谱、安全驱动优化先例），并设置显式策略和人类审查检查点来确保可审计性和可证伪性。

Result: 提出了一种人机协同的AI-for-science工作流框架，使生成的假设保持可审计、可证伪，并受先验药理学知识的约束。

Conclusion: PRECEDE提供了一种有前景的范式，将AI生成能力与人类监督相结合，实现安全感知的药物重新设计，确保在药理学知识和人类专家审查的边界内运作。

Abstract: We propose PRECEDE, a precedent-guided co-scientist for side-effect-aware drug redesign that revises a parent compound to mitigate a specified side effect while preserving therapeutic function. Rather than isolated molecular generation, PRECEDE frames redesign as evidence-grounded reasoning over drug--side-effect associations, biomedical knowledge graphs, and precedents of safety-driven optimization, coordinated by an LLM orchestrator with explicit policies and human-review checkpoints. We position PRECEDE as a human-supervised AI-for-science workflow in which hypotheses remain auditable, falsifiable, and bounded by prior pharmacology.

</details>


### [11] [Individual Parameters in Weight-Sparse Transformers Appear Interpretable](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02964)
*Arnau Marin-Llobet,Stefan Heimersheim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种自动化LLM流水线，用于判断单个权重在全局输入分布中何时重要，并为每个权重生成可泛化的自然语言描述。实验发现，稀疏Transformer中可解释权重的比例（12-31%）高于密集Transformer，且这一差距在剔除不可靠描述后进一步扩大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可解释性方法通常针对特定行为逆向工程组件功能，但忽略了同一组件在不同输入子集上可能有不同功能。作者希望从全局角度理解单个权重在完整训练分布上何时起作用，从而更全面地揭示权重的功能。

Method: 设计一个自动化LLM流水线：首先通过消融实验识别权重改变模型预测的输入；然后让LLM生成关于“该权重何时重要”的简短人类可读描述；最后在留出文本上验证该描述是否泛化，只有通过验证的权重才被认定为可解释。实验在两种稀疏Transformer和两种密集Transformer上进行。

Result: 稀疏Transformer中12-31%的权重拥有单个简短描述能标识其用途，且这一比例高于密集Transformer；剔除不可靠描述后，稀疏与密集模型之间的可解释性差距进一步扩大。

Conclusion: 单个权重可以以全局方式被理解，且稀疏Transformer比密集Transformer具有更高比例的可解释权重。这表明稀疏架构有助于提升权重的功能可解释性，为模型设计和可解释性研究提供了新视角。

Abstract: A central goal of mechanistic interpretability is to understand how neural networks work and what each individual component does. Dominant circuit-finding approaches focus on a specific behavior and reverse-engineer the role of components on the associated sub-distribution. However, past work has shown that components can have different functions that are active on different subsets of the input distribution. In this work we ask whether a single weight can be understood globally across the full training distribution by characterizing when it matters (the inputs on which ablating it changes the model's predictions). We introduce an automated LLM pipeline that writes a short, human-readable description of when a weight matters and verifies it on held-out text, crediting a weight only if its description generalizes. Across two sparse and two dense transformers, the fraction of weights that are interpretable (in this sense) is higher in sparse transformers than in dense ones, a gap that widens once unreliable descriptions are discarded. Our results show that a meaningful fraction of a sparse transformer model's weights can be interpreted: 12 to 31% of weights have a single short description that identifies what the weight is used for.

</details>


### [12] [LACE-SVD: Loss-Aware SVD with Cumulative Error Correction for LLM Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03057)
*Zhuowen Liu,Longkun Hao,Shiyu Feng,Xiaowen Chang,Ruiqun Li,Changqun Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出LACE-SVD框架，通过损失感知的秩预算分配和累积误差校正，解决低秩压缩中忽略层间敏感性和误差传播的问题，在LLaMA-7B高压缩比下显著优于Dobi-SVD。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SVD压缩方法依赖局部重建目标，未考虑层间损失敏感性差异，且局部近似误差经残差流累积放大，导致全局偏离原始模型。

Method: 1) 估计候选压缩比对应的校准负对数似然增量，求解预算约束分配问题确定秩预算；2) 通过闭式局部更新优化压缩模型；3) 引入传播感知校正模块，减少残差流输出层的层输出差异以抑制累积误差传播。

Result: 在LLaMA-7B上压缩比0.6时，WikiText-2困惑度32.57，显著优于Dobi-SVD的46.18。

Conclusion: LACE-SVD通过联合考虑损失敏感性和误差传播，有效提升了大模型低秩压缩的性能，尤其在高压缩比场景下优势明显。

Abstract: The rapid growth in the parameter scale of large language models (LLMs) has created a strong demand for efficient compression techniques. As a hardware-agnostic and highly compatible approach, low-rank compression has been widely adopted to reduce both memory footprint and computational cost. However, existing SVD-based methods are still largely driven by local reconstruction objectives, overlooking two critical limitations: rank budgets are often allocated without explicitly considering layer-wise loss sensitivity, and local approximation errors can propagate and accumulate through the residual stream, leading to amplified global deviations from the original model. To address these issues, we propose LACE-SVD, a Loss-Aware SVD framework with Cumulative Error correction for LLM compression. LACE-SVD first estimates the calibration negative-log-likelihood increase induced by candidate layer-wise compression ratios and solves a budget-constrained allocation problem to assign rank budgets. It then refines the compressed model with closed-form local updates and introduces a propagation-aware correction for residual-stream output modules, reducing layer-output discrepancy as a proxy for cumulative error propagation. Experimental results demonstrate that at a high compression ratio (0.6), the WikiText-2 PPL of our method on LLaMA-7B (32.57) is significantly better than that of Dobi-SVD (46.18).

</details>


### [13] [STELLA: Efficient Sensor-to-LLM Translation for On-Device Human Activity Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03089)
*Nirhoshan Sivaroopan,Albert Zomaya,Kanchana Thilakarathna*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出STELLA框架，通过轻量级分层传感器分词器将多通道惯性数据压缩为紧凑潜变量令牌，输入冻结的预训练LLM进行标签评分，实现边缘设备上高效、准确、隐私保护的持续人类活动识别，在7个数据集上达到SOTA，F1最多提升11.83%，个性化可再提升21.91%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的HAR方法难以在边缘设备上持续部署：原始传感器提示过长、云端推理引入延迟和隐私风险、微调LLM将通用模型退化为特定任务分类器。需要一种既能利用LLM能力又适合边缘部署的高效方案。

Method: STELLA采用轻量级分层分词器，将整个多通道惯性窗口压缩为固定数量的紧凑潜变量传感器令牌，并将其投影到冻结的预训练LLM嵌入空间，与自然语言提示结合进行标签评分。支持仅更新分词器的设备端个性化，并利用本地检索上下文增强推理。

Result: 在7个公开HAR数据集和8个基准设置上达到SOTA，相比先前方法F1提升最多11.83%；设备端个性化可在用户数据积累后进一步提升最多21.91% F1；优于代表性时间序列分词器；在移动和边缘设备上实现实时推理。

Conclusion: 高效的传感器分词是实现准确、隐私保护且个性化的边缘设备LLM-HAR的实用路径，将负担从LLM适配转移到传感器令牌化。

Abstract: HAR is increasingly expected to run continuously on edge devices, yet recent LLM-based methods remain hard to deploy: raw sensor prompts are long, cloud inference adds latency and privacy risk, and fine-tuned LLM pipelines turn general-purpose models into task-specific classifiers. We present STELLA, an efficient sensor-to-LLM translation framework for on-device HAR that shifts the burden from LLM adaptation to sensor tokenization. A lightweight hierarchical tokenizer compresses an entire multi-channel inertial window into a fixed set of compact latent sensor tokens, which are projected into the embedding space of a frozen pretrained LLM and combined with a natural-language prompt for label scoring. This preserves activity-relevant temporal and cross-channel structure while keeping LLM-side computation predictable across sensor configurations. STELLA also supports on-device personalization, adapting only the lightweight tokenizer on small amounts of user-specific labelled data and augmenting inference with a local retrieval context, keeping the LLM, user data, and retrieval on device. Across seven public HAR datasets and eight benchmark settings, STELLA achieves new state-of-the-art performance, improving over prior methods by up to 11.83% F1; on-device personalization yields up to a further 21.91% F1 as user data accumulates after deployment. STELLA also outperforms representative time-series tokenizers under the same LLM pipeline and achieves real-time inference under practical mobile and edge budgets, showing that efficient sensor tokenization is a practical path toward accurate, private, and personalized LLM-based HAR on edge devices.

</details>


### [14] [Sample-Efficient Pareto Front Modeling for Energy-Aware Reinforcement Learning Using Bayesian Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03140)
*Georg Schäfer,Jakob Rehrl,Stefan Huber,Simon Hirlaender*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于多目标贝叶斯优化（MOBO）的自动化权重选择方法，用于在强化学习框架中发现能量感知控制的最优帕累托前沿，在Quanser Aero 2平台上验证了其优于均匀网格搜索的样本效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对工业自动化中多目标强化学习依赖人工权重调参、耗时且易引入偏差、难以发现最优权衡解的问题，自动化权重选择过程以系统高效地发现帕累托前沿。

Method: 将权重选择过程建模为多目标贝叶斯优化（MOBO）问题，采用期望超体积改进（qEHVI）采集函数，在1-DoF俯仰控制的Quanser Aero 2平台上进行实验，以均匀网格搜索为基线对比样本效率。

Result: MOBO方法在超体积和最大展宽指标上一致优于均匀网格采样，能以更少的评估预算识别出高质量、多样化的权衡策略。

Conclusion: MOBO方法实现了高效的能量感知控制，适用于复杂机电系统中自动化权重选择与帕累托最优策略的发现。

Abstract: Industrial automation increasingly demands control strategies that balance operational performance with strict energy efficiency requirements. A common approach to solving this multi-objective problem, particularly within the framework of reinforcement learning (RL), is to formulate a single, scalar reward function that linearly combines the competing objectives. However, the manual weighting of these different objectives is heavily reliant on domain intuition, incredibly time-consuming, prone to human bias, and frequently fails to uncover optimal trade-off solutions. This work addresses the critical challenge of automating the weight selection process to systematically and efficiently discover the Pareto front of optimal trade-off policies. We formulate the weight selection process as a multi-objective Bayesian optimization (MOBO) problem and evaluate its sample efficiency against a standard uniform grid search baseline. Using a physical Quanser Aero 2 testbed configured for 1-DoF pitch control, our results demonstrate that the MOBO approach, utilizing the expected hypervolume improvement (qEHVI) acquisition function, consistently outperforms uniform grid sampling. MOBO achieves superior hypervolume and maximum spread, successfully identifying high-quality, diverse trade-off policies with a reduced evaluation budget, thereby enabling highly efficient energy-aware control in complex mechatronic systems.

</details>


### [15] [From Global to Local: Efficient Regional Weather Downscaling with Global Weather Foundation Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03279)
*Wiktor Kamzela,Jakub Kubiak,Adam Dobosz,Jędrzej Miczke,Anatol Kaczmarek,Piotr Wyrwiński,Wojciech Stefaniak,Wojciech Kotłowski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于预训练天气基础模型的降尺度框架，通过在预训练骨干网络的潜在空间中添加轻量级多尺度预测头，实现全球预报的区域精细化，无需重新训练骨干网络。该方法以区域数值模拟为训练目标，在网格分辨率提升两个数量级的情况下仍有效，计算成本远低于传统NWP，且在多数指标上优于传统数值天气预报和基于图像的超分辨率方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统有限区域模型计算成本高，而现有学习方法将问题简化为超分辨率，忽略了跨尺度的统计和物理失配问题。需要一种既能保持全球动力一致性又能高效解析精细尺度结构的降尺度方法。

Method: 基于预训练天气基础模型（骨干网络）的潜在空间，添加轻量级多尺度预测头进行区域适应。骨干网络保持冻结，仅在潜在空间中学习区域精化映射。以区域数值模拟数据为训练目标，并在网格数据之外额外使用地面气象站观测进行系统偏差分析。

Result: 在大多数评估指标上优于传统数值天气预报（NWP），且计算成本仅为NWP的一小部分。基于预训练天气基础模型潜在空间的方法优于标准基于图像的超分辨率方法。能够实现网格分辨率两个数量级提升的有效降尺度。

Conclusion: 基于预训练全球天气基础模型的潜在空间进行区域降尺度是一种高效且准确的方法，能够弥合全球再分析、区域模拟和地面观测之间的系统偏差，为低成本高分辨率的区域天气预报提供了新范式。

Abstract: Accurate regional weather prediction requires resolving fine-scale structure while remaining consistent with global dynamics. Traditional limited area models rely on computationally expensive simulations, while many learning-based approaches frame the problem as super-resolution, overlooking statistical and physical mismatches across scales. We propose a foundation-model-driven downscaling framework that learns regional refinements of global forecasts by augmenting a pretrained weather model backbone with lightweight, multi-scale prediction heads operating directly in its latent space. Despite being trained on substantially coarser inputs, the pretrained backbone supports regional adaptation at resolutions corresponding to a two-order-of-magnitude increase in grid-cell resolution, without the need for retraining. The proposed approach uses regional numerical simulations as training targets and is evaluated not only against gridded datasets but also against ground-based weather station observations, enabling analysis of systematic biases between global reanalysis, regional simulations, and in-situ weather station observations. Our experiments show improved accuracy in comparison to NWP on most of the metrics at the fraction of computational cost. Moreover, we observe that building on a latent space of globally pre-trained weather foundation model offers better downscaling capabilities than the standard image-based super-resolution approaches.

</details>


### [16] [Amortising Bayesian Experimental Design for Sequential Information Gathering in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03426)
*Jakob Hartmann,James Harvey,Jhonathan Navott,Erik Y. Wang,Luckeciano C. Melo,Flaviu Cipcigan,Cheng Zhang,Alessandro Abate*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ASIG，一种通过多轮GRPO和期望信息增益奖励将贝叶斯实验设计摊销到LLM策略中的微调方法，在20 Questions任务上使7B基座模型成功率翻倍，推理成本降低25倍以上，并在医疗诊断基准MediQ上展现出跨分布迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM虽具备强大的推理和世界知识能力，但在需要多轮交互的序贯决策场景中，难以有效收集信息，现有方法（如推理时BED）计算成本高。

Method: 提出ASIG方法，通过多轮扩展的GRPO（Group Relative Policy Optimisation）算法，以期望信息增益（Expected Information Gain）作为奖励信号，将贝叶斯实验设计（BED）摊销到LLM策略中。

Result: 在20 Questions任务中，ASIG使7B基座模型成功率翻倍，推理成本相比BED-LLM降低25倍以上；在未见过的医疗诊断基准MediQ上，7B规模模型的信息获取性能也得到提升。

Conclusion: 将BED摊销到LLM策略中是实现高效序贯信息收集的有效且计算高效的方法，学到的策略具备跨分布迁移能力。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) exhibit strong reasoning and world-knowledge capabilities, yet often struggle to gather information effectively across the multi-turn interactions required in sequential decision-making settings. We introduce Amortised Sequential Information Gathering (ASIG), a fine-tuning approach that amortises Bayesian Experimental Design (BED) into LLM policies via a multi-turn extension of Group Relative Policy Optimisation with an Expected Information Gain reward. Evaluated on the 20 Questions task, ASIG more than doubles the success rate of the 7B base model and reduces inference cost by over $25\times$ relative to BED-LLM, a competitive inference-time baseline. Applied to MediQ, a medical diagnosis benchmark unseen during training, ASIG improves information-seeking performance at the 7B scale, suggesting that the learned strategies can transfer out of distribution. Our findings show that amortising BED into LLM policies provides an effective and computationally efficient approach to sequential information gathering.

</details>


### [17] [No Time Like the Present: Agentic Test-Time Training for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03441)
*Yanbo Wang,Jinhua Hao,Yuze Shi,Kun Yuan,Ming Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Agentic Test-Time Training (aTTT)，一种在长程多轮智能体交互中通过token级重加权来抑制重复n-gram损失、防止策略漂移的连续测试时训练方法，在ALFWorld和SWE-bench Lite上分别提升最多5.0和4.9个百分点的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体在长轨迹中性能退化：重复探索已访问状态、重复失败动作、丢失有效策略。现有TTT方法多为单次静态适应，无法应对多轮交互中策略变化导致的漂移问题。

Method: 提出aTTT，在连续多轮TTT中检测更新文本中的重复n-gram，对重复出现的token进行损失降权，对新颖token保持全权重。基于vLLM的运行时LoRA API构建并发服务系统，降低开销至无TTT成本的1.9倍。

Result: ALFWorld上成功率提升最多5.0个百分点，SWE-bench Lite上提升最多4.9个百分点。增益主要集中在模型已具备任务能力但因长轨迹漂移的场景，表明aTTT主要保留已有能力而非教授新技能。

Conclusion: aTTT通过缓解重复文本导致的训练漂移，有效维持智能体在长轨迹中的已有能力，为长程LLM智能体部署提供了轻量级、可并行的TTT方案。

Abstract: LLM agents often degrade over long episodes: as trajectories grow, they revisit explored states, repeat failed actions, and lose strategies that previously worked. Test-time training (TTT) offers a way to adapt model weights to the evolving task state, but existing LLM TTT methods largely adapt once to a fixed input. We study continuous TTT in multi-turn agent episodes, where each update changes the policy that generates later training text. This creates a self-training loop that helps when new trajectory information appears, but can amplify drift when the agent gets stuck and repeatedly trains on similar text. We find that update-text repetition distinguishes these regimes and introduce Agentic Test-Time Training (aTTT), a token-level reweighting method that downweights the loss on tokens appearing in repeated $n$-grams from prior updates while leaving novel tokens fully weighted. To run such updates inside live episodes, we build a concurrent serving system using vLLM's runtime LoRA API, limiting overhead to 1.9$\times$ the no-TTT cost. aTTT improves success by up to 5.0 points on ALFWorld and 4.9 points on SWE-bench Lite. The gains concentrate where models already have task competence but drift over long trajectories, suggesting that aTTT mainly preserves existing competence rather than teaching new abilities.

</details>


### [18] [Best-of-Better-$N$: Generating Pre-Aligned Responses with In-Context Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03453)
*Eric Lei,Hsiang Hsu,Chun-Fu Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Best-of-Better-N (BoBN)方法，通过检索高奖励示例并进行风格重写，利用上下文学习提升参考LLM生成高质量响应的概率，从而增强推理时对齐方法（如Best-of-N）的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理时对齐方法（如Best-of-N）的效果受限于参考LLM自身的响应质量——如果参考LLM对高奖励响应的生成概率极低，则任何选择策略都无法成功找到对齐输出。因此需要一种方法提升参考LLM生成高质量响应的概率。

Method: 提出BoBN框架：1) 检索与输入查询和任务相关的高奖励示例；2) 通过参考LLM对检索到的响应进行风格重写（restyling），使其符合目标任务的格式和风格；3) 将重写后的示例作为上下文提示，引导参考LLM的采样分布向高奖励区域偏移。

Result: 在安全对齐和数学推理基准上，BoBN相比基线方法在固定N时取得更好性能，且达到目标性能所需的N更小。理论上证明了上下文学习能够将预训练Transformer的输出分布向高奖励区域偏移。

Conclusion: BoBN通过上下文学习有效提升了参考LLM生成高奖励响应的概率，从而增强了推理时对齐方法的实际效果，在固定采样预算下实现了更优的对齐性能。

Abstract: Inference-time alignment methods, such as Best-of-$N$, offer a flexible alternative to training-based alignment by using reward models to select high-quality responses generated by a reference LLM. However, the efficacy of these methods is inherently limited by the response quality: if the reference LLM assigns negligible probability to high-reward responses, no selection strategy will succeed in finding aligned outputs. In this work, we propose Best-of-Better-$N$ (BoBN), an in context learning-based generation framework to address this challenge. Our method utilizes retrieval from high-reward examples relevant to the input query and task. Crucially, we introduce a restyling step where retrieved responses are rewritten by the reference LLM to align with the target task's format and style. These restyled examples are used in-context to shift the sampling distribution toward the high-reward region. We analytically characterize how in-context learning shifts the output distribution of pretrained transformers toward the high-reward region, resulting in provable benefits on the target task. We then evaluate BoBN on safety alignment and mathematical reasoning benchmarks across several reference LLMs. BoBN's higher-quality responses enable better performance to be achieved when the number of responses $N$ is fixed, and smaller $N$ required to achieve a target performance.

</details>


### [19] [Aligning Language Models with Selective Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03528)
*Gaoxiang Luo,Yifan Wu,Sinian Zhang,Aryan Deshwal,Ju Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RLSR框架，通过在LLM后训练对齐阶段优化风险覆盖曲线下面积（AURC）指标，实现选择性预测以提升LLM可靠性，在域内和域外任务上均优于现有对齐方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在关键决策场景中可靠性不足，现有对齐方法主要关注正确性或校准指标，缺乏对选择性预测性能（即风险-覆盖权衡）的针对性优化。

Method: 提出强化学习选择奖励（RLSR）框架，以风险覆盖曲线下面积（AURC）为对齐目标，通过强化学习优化LLM的选择性预测性能。

Result: RLSR在域内和域外任务上均实现了显著优于多个对齐基线的风险-覆盖权衡。

Conclusion: 通过将选择性预测指标纳入对齐目标，RLSR有效提升了LLM的可靠性，为人机协作提供了更好的风险控制方案。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed as critical decision-making components in high-stakes real-world AI systems, rendering LLM reliability a foremost practical concern. In this paper, we focus on enhancing LLM reliability through selective prediction (SP), a strategy that allows an LLM to only predict for inputs where it is likely to be correct (i.e., coverage) and hence reduce the error rate (i.e., risk) on that portion of inputs -- flagging the remaining inputs for future human discretion. In other words, SP improves LLM reliability by balancing the risk-coverage trade-off and enabling seamless human-AI collaboration. To integrate SP into LLMs, we focus on the LLM post-training alignment stage and propose to align LLMs with SP performance metrics, in contrast with existing LLM alignment methods that focus primarily on correctness or calibration metrics. Specifically, we propose a novel alignment framework, Reinforcement Learning for Selection Reward (RLSR), which targets the area under the risk-coverage curve (AURC) -- a popular SP performance metric -- as its alignment objective. RLSR achieves substantially better risk-coverage trade-off compared to multiple alignment baselines on both in-domain and out-of-domain tasks.

</details>


### [20] [Differentiate the Evaluator, Not the Program: An Efficient Runtime Representation for Neuro-Symbolic Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03574)
*Lucas Sheneman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出原生可微分虚拟机（NDVM），通过将符号结构与可微分数值状态分离，在运行时对可执行程序进行微分，无需为每个候选程序编译独立计算图，实现程序-参数联合搜索的高效加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在程序与参数的联合搜索中，参数校准成为瓶颈：外层循环生成大量候选程序，每个程序都需要内层基于梯度的优化才能评估。现有方法要么为每个候选构建独立可微图（牺牲程序即数据的灵活性），要么采用解释器方式（解释器开销占据主导）。需要一种既能保持程序即数据特性又能高效微分的运行时表示。

Method: NDVM将符号结构（标签、符号、环境、控制流）作为原生运行时数据保留，而将数值载荷组织在密集批处理缓冲区中，沿实际执行轨迹记录精确的逆向模式梯度。通过单一求值器遍历，将微分开销均摊到大量参数向量上。设计基于真实可微自托管Scheme解释器的锁定成本模型。

Result: 与参考后端相比，NDVM实现了约60x的每通道批处理均摊、近线性多核扩展、两个独立前端；在固定预算的LLM提议程序联合搜索中，NDVM达到高质量解的速度约快24倍（墙钟时间）。

Conclusion: 运行时微分作为科学发现工作流的实用系统基础是可行的，NDVM有效解决了程序-参数联合搜索中的参数校准瓶颈问题。

Abstract: AI systems increasingly propose executable scientific models whose value depends on both their symbolic structure and their fitted continuous parameters. This makes parameter calibration the bottleneck of program-and-parameter co-search: an outer loop can generate thousands of candidate programs, but each needs an inner gradient-based optimization before it can be assessed. Staging each candidate into its own differentiable graph makes individual models fast but sacrifices the program-as-data property that keeps search fluid; interpreter-based approaches preserve programs as runtime data but pay interpreter overhead that dominates the numerical work.
  We present the Native Differentiable Virtual Machine (NDVM), a runtime representation that differentiates executable programs without compiling each candidate into a separate graph. NDVM separates symbolic structure from differentiable numeric state: tags, symbols, environments, and control remain native runtime data, while numeric payloads live in dense batched buffers with exact reverse-mode gradients recorded along the realized execution trace, so one evaluator walk is amortized across large populations of parameter vectors. A locked cost model of a real differentiable self-hosted Scheme interpreter motivates the design. We realize NDVM as a native runtime with forward and gradient equivalence to the reference backend, about 60x per-lane batch amortization, near-linear multicore scaling, and two independent front ends. In fixed-budget co-search over LLM-proposed programs, NDVM reaches high-quality solutions about 24x sooner in wall-clock time, suggesting runtime differentiation as a practical systems foundation for scientific discovery workflows.

</details>


### [21] [Modular Foundation Models for Time-Series Perception in Digital Twins](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03585)
*Quang Hung Pham,Ryad Zemouri,Martin Gagnon,Luc Vouligny*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于预训练表示编码器集合的模块化基础模型，用于时间序列感知，通过自监督学习、门控机制和Transformer自注意力聚合，支持插补、长期预测和少样本学习等多项PHM任务，并在ETT基准和工业水轮发电机虚拟传感案例中验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列感知方法多为任务特定、数据密集且难以扩展，纯数据驱动模型在不同工况下缺乏鲁棒性和可迁移性，亟需一种可复用、跨任务的模块化基础模型。

Method: 构建预训练表示编码器集合，通过自监督学习在异构数据集上学习任务无关的可迁移表示；引入门控机制动态选择相关编码器；利用Transformer自注意力模块建模跨编码器交互，将选中的表示投影到共享潜空间并聚合；下游任务通过轻量级任务头实现，预训练编码器保持冻结。

Result: 在ETT基准上展示了跨任务的竞争性性能；工业案例（水轮发电机转子温度虚拟传感）验证了实际应用价值；消融实验证实了自监督预训练、编码器选择、表示对齐和自适应聚合的互补作用。

Conclusion: 该模块化基础模型有效提升了时间序列感知的迁移性和鲁棒性，为PHM系统提供了一种可扩展的通用框架。

Abstract: Engineering Digital Twins and Prognostics and Health Management (PHM) systems rely on robust perception modules to extract actionable information from heterogeneous and non-stationary time-series data. However, most existing approaches remain task-specific, data-hungry, and difficult to integrate into scalable monitoring and decision-making pipelines. Moreover, purely data-driven models often lack robustness and transferability across varying operating conditions. To address these challenges, this paper proposes a modular foundation model for time-series perception based on a collection of pretrained representation encoders. The framework leverages self-supervised learning on heterogeneous datasets to learn transferable and task-agnostic representations, which can be reused across multiple PHM tasks. A gating mechanism is introduced to dynamically select relevant encoders for a given target dataset, enabling conditional computation and adaptive model composition. The selected representations are projected into a shared latent space and aggregated using a Transformer-based self-attention module that explicitly models cross-encoder interactions. The resulting architecture supports multiple downstream tasks, including imputation, long-term forecasting, and few-shot learning, through lightweight task-specific heads, while keeping pretrained encoders frozen during adaptation. Extensive ablation studies demonstrate the complementary roles of self-supervised pretraining, encoder selection, representation alignment, and adaptive aggregation. Experimental results on the ETT benchmark show competitive performance across tasks, while a real-world industrial case study on virtual sensing for hydro-generator rotor temperature highlights the practical relevance of the approach.

</details>


### [22] [Graph Classification via Network Usable Information: From Representation Evaluation to Structure Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03587)
*Abdullah Shaik,Anwar Said*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: NetinfoGC提出了一种基于网络可用信息（NUI）范式的图分类框架，利用传播机制和经典结构描述符（如图中心性度量）构建置换不变的图表示，并通过无训练的NUI估计和稀疏组LASSO正则化实现表示质量评估与自动特征选择。实验表明经典中心性度量与学习得到的传播表示竞争力相当，且NUI与分类准确率强相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统图神经网络依赖端到端训练的黑盒嵌入，缺乏可解释性和表示质量的先验评估手段。作者希望构建一个统一、可解释且无需端到端神经训练的图分类框架，同时引入一种无监督的表示质量评估方法。

Method: 1) 基于传播机制和经典结构描述符（如图中心性度量）构建置换不变的图表示族；2) 引入基于聚类一致性的无训练NUI估计方法，作为任务相关信息的代理指标；3) 使用稀疏组LASSO正则化自动选择信息性结构描述符并抑制冗余特征。

Result: 基准数据集实验表明：经典中心性度量与学习得到的传播表示性能相当甚至更优；估计的NUI与下游分类准确率之间存在强相关性。

Conclusion: NetinfoGC提供了一个统一且可解释的图表示评估与利用框架，无需端到端神经训练即可有效进行图分类，且NUI可作为表示效用的有效衡量指标。

Abstract: We propose NetinfoGC, a framework for graph classification that extends the Network Usable Information (NUI) paradigm to graph-level learning. Unlike conventional graph neural network approaches that rely on end-to-end training of black-box embeddings, NetinfoGC constructs a family of permutation-invariant graph representations derived from propagation-based mechanisms and classical structural descriptors, including graph centrality measures.
  To evaluate representation quality, we introduce a training-free NUI estimation procedure based on clustering consistency with ground-truth labels, providing a proxy for task-relevant information without supervised learning. We further exploit the same representations using sparse-group LASSO regularization, enabling automatic selection of informative structural descriptors while suppressing redundant ones.
  Experiments on benchmark datasets show that classical centrality measures are highly competitive with learned propagation-based representations, and in several cases yield superior performance. Moreover, we observe a strong correlation between estimated NUI and downstream classification accuracy, validating NUI as an effective measure of representation utility.
  Overall, NetinfoGC provides a unified and interpretable framework for evaluating and exploiting graph representations without requiring end-to-end neural training.

</details>


### [23] [LLM-Guided Transportation Hub Capacity Planning with Textual Business Inputs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03651)
*Xiaoyue Liu,Zheng Dong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种LLM智能体框架，通过链式思维推理将定性业务上下文转化为枢纽容量决策，与优化模型形成反馈循环。在13枢纽货运网络中，该框架实现了2.8%的最优性差距，显著优于传统优化模型的11.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统枢纽容量规划模型虽能有效优化定量输入，但无法消化定性业务上下文（如自然语言描述的商业洞察），导致决策质量受限。

Method: 提出LLM智能体框架：1) 链式思维推理协议，将每项上下文信息映射为具体容量调整的方向与幅度，构建结构化决策表；2) 与优化模型形成反馈循环，使用基于路径的绩效指标验证并引导智能体选择最佳容量决策。

Result: 在美国东南部13枢纽货运网络真实案例中，该框架达到2.8%的最优性差距，相比传统无文本输入优化模型的11.0%提升了8.2个百分点。

Conclusion: LLM可作为上下文桥梁，将定性业务洞察有效整合到运筹学工作流中，显著提升枢纽容量规划的质量。

Abstract: While traditional hub capacity planning models optimize effectively for quantitative inputs, they often fail to digest qualitative business context. We propose a novel framework where a large language model (LLM) agent iteratively proposes hub capacity decisions guided by natural-language business context descriptions. The key mechanism is a chain-of-thought reasoning protocol: the LLM constructs a structured decision table that maps each contextual item to specific capacity adjustments based on the implied direction and magnitude of changes. The new capacity decision is then validated through a feedback loop with an optimization model, which provides routing-based performance metrics to guide the agent's selection. On a real-world 13-hub freight network in the southeastern US, our framework achieves a 2.8% optimality gap relative to the hidden ground-truth, a significant improvement over the 11.0% gap produced by the traditional optimization model without textual business inputs. This demonstrates that LLMs can serve as a contextual bridge, integrating qualitative business insights into Operations Research workflows.

</details>


### [24] [Social Networks of LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03695)
*Kaixuan Liu,Guojun Xiong,Weinan Zhang,Shengpu Tang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SNLA框架，研究LLM智能体在社交网络中的集体信念形成机制，发现窄注意力导致羊群效应而宽注意力在特定条件下能恢复群体智慧。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典社交网络模型假设网络结构决定信念融合，但LLM智能体的有限注意力使其只能接收部分信息，导致这些模型高估了信息聚合程度，无法区分真正共识和羊群效应。

Method: 提出SNLA框架，建模每个智能体对其他智能体的实际影响力（而非仅网络连接），该影响力取决于智能体在网络中的位置和注意力聚焦程度。在可处理代理模型上进行理论分析，并通过受控测试台和多智能体LLM基准验证。

Result: 理论表明：窄注意力导致羊群效应，有效样本量随群体规模增长有界；宽注意力仅在无向且度正则的暴露图上恢复群体智慧。受控测试台直接验证了预测，羊群-智慧转变在三个多智能体LLM基准的变体上复现。

Conclusion: LLM智能体群体的信念聚合结果高度依赖于注意力范围和网络结构，窄注意力会导致虚假共识（羊群效应），而宽注意力也仅在特定网络条件下才能实现真正的群体智慧。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents are increasingly deployed in interacting populations, raising the question of what such populations come to believe collectively. Whether a population aggregates genuine knowledge or collapses into a false consensus directly affects how much such systems can be trusted. Classical social-network models assume that the network itself determines how beliefs combine. This assumption breaks down for LLM agents, whose limited attention takes in only part of what they are exposed to, so these models overstate how much information a population actually pools and cannot tell genuine consensus from herding. We introduce SNLA, a framework that models how much each agent actually influences others, rather than merely how the network connects them. This influence depends on each agent's position in the network and on how sharply attention focuses. Theoretically, we show on a tractable proxy that narrow attention causes herding, where the effective sample size stays bounded regardless of population size, while wide attention recovers wisdom-of-crowds behavior only when the exposure graph is undirected and degree-regular. Empirically, a controlled testbed validates these predictions directly, and the herding-wisdom transition reproduces on operator-controlled variants of three multi-agent LLM benchmarks.

</details>


### [25] [FedACT: Federated Adaptive Coordinate Trust Modulation for Robust Transformer Training under Data Heterogeneity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03763)
*Shuai Li,Qinglin Wang,Ping Luo,Jiahuan Wang,Hongyang Hu,Haotian Mo,Yigui Feng,Ziang Liu,Qisong Xiao,Jie Liu,Tao Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FedACT，一种用于联邦AdamW训练的全局感知坐标信任调制方法，通过按坐标分配更新幅度来解决异构数据下的坐标信任不匹配问题，在视觉Transformer、CNN、LLM预训练和微调等任务上均优于现有联邦自适应优化方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦自适应优化器主要在客户端更新或通信轮次层面处理信任不匹配问题，但在坐标维度上仍然均匀地应用修正后的自适应方向，忽略了不同坐标在可靠性上的差异。

Method: FedACT首先生成全局修正的自适应方向，然后根据坐标级别的信任得分重新分配更新幅度：对局部梯度和全局修正共同支持的坐标分配较大步长，对剩余坐标保留较小的非零更新。

Result: 在联邦视觉Transformer、CNN、LLM预训练和LLM微调上的大量实验表明，FedACT在强联邦自适应基线上持续改进，在更强的数据异构性和Transformer模型上取得了最大增益。

Conclusion: 机制分析表明FedACT改善了跨客户端方向一致性，坐标级别的信任分配有效补充了轮次级别的全局-局部修正。

Abstract: Federated Transformer training increasingly relies on local AdamW, whose adaptive updates can provide much stronger local progress than SGD-based training. However, under heterogeneous client data, even globally corrected AdamW updates may remain highly uneven in coordinate-wise reliability. We refer to this phenomenon as coordinate trust mismatch. Existing federated adaptive optimizers mainly address mismatch at the client-update or communication-round level, but still apply the corrected adaptive direction densely and uniformly across coordinates. In this paper, we propose FedACT, a global-aware coordinate trust modulation method for federated AdamW training. FedACT first forms a globally corrected adaptive direction and then reallocates update magnitudes according to a coordinate-wise trust score, assigning larger steps to coordinates jointly supported by local gradients and global correction, while preserving smaller non-zero updates on the remaining coordinates. Extensive experiments on federated vision Transformers, CNNs, LLM pre-training, and LLM fine-tuning show that FedACT consistently improves over strong federated adaptive baselines, with the largest gains on Transformer models under stronger data heterogeneity. Mechanism analyses further show that FedACT improves cross-client direction consistency, suggesting that coordinate-level trust allocation effectively complements round-level global-local correction. Code will be released.

</details>


### [26] [Foundations of Equivariant Deep Learning: Unifying Graph and Sheaf Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03798)
*Yoshihiro Maruyama*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了阶等变神经网络（OENN），通过面偏序集上的等变丛理论将几何深度学习推广到更丰富的对称结构，并进一步扩展到范畴等变神经网络（CENN），建立了等变神经网络的一般形式及通用逼近定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 几何深度学习利用数据中的对称性来提升深度学习系统的性能与效率，但现有方法未能充分利用更丰富的对称结构。本文旨在扩展几何深度学习，使其能够利用更复杂的对称性。

Method: 基于面偏序集（face posets）上的等变丛理论，开发了阶等变神经网络（OENN）。具体包括：(i) 刻画所有线性阶等变映射；(ii) 构建OENN层；(iii) 证明连续阶等变映射的通用逼近定理。进一步将OENN扩展为范畴等变神经网络（CENN）。

Result: 完整刻画了线性阶等变映射，构建了OENN层，并证明了连续阶等变映射的通用逼近定理（该结果即使限制在层图神经网络上也是新的）。同时展示了OENN在图和层模型上的应用，并将已知的GNN通用逼近定理推广到了更一般的框架中。CENN的建立给出了等变神经网络的一般形式。

Conclusion: OENN成功将几何深度学习推广到更丰富的对称结构，且CENN进一步引入了范畴对称性（如多个对象上的非可逆对称性及对称间的组合关系），为利用更一般的数据对称性提供了理论框架。

Abstract: Symmetry is everywhere in nature and society. Geometric deep learning exploits symmetries in data to improve the performance and efficiency of deep learning systems. In this paper, we extend geometric deep learning to utilize richer symmetry structures. Specifically, we develop order-equivariant neural networks (OENN), which generalize standard graph message passing and sheaf neural networks via the theory of equivariant bundles over face posets (face categories). We (i) characterize all linear order-equivariant maps, (ii) build OENN layers, and (iii) prove universal approximation theorems (UATs) for continuous order-equivariant maps, which are new results even when restricted to sheaf neural networks (for which no UAT was known before). We illustrate the framework on graph and sheaf models. Our results can also be seen as extending the known UAT for graph neural networks to a more general setting that subsumes sheaf neural networks as well. In addition, we show that OENN can be extended further to CENN, Category-Equivariant Neural Network, which gives the general form of equivariant neural networks as well as of equivariant universal approximation theorems, allowing us to leverage categorical symmetry in data (e.g., non-invertible symmetries on multiple objects with compositional relations on those symmetries).

</details>


### [27] [NeSy-CSA: A Neuro-Symbolic Framework for Open-Ended Critical Scenario Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03847)
*Qitong Chu,Xunjie He,Chen Deng,Huaxin Pei,Yufeng Yue*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出NeSy-CSA，一个神经符号框架，将开放性的关键场景归因从无约束的解释生成转变为结构化、可溯源的推理过程，在四个决策环境中相比LLM基线在干预有效性指标上分别提升18.32%和13.67%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 场景测试中发现的关键场景的归因是一个开放性问题，现有归因方法难以兼顾开放推理的灵活性与关键场景推理所需的可解释性和可溯源性。

Method: NeSy-CSA通过选择相关因子缩小归因空间，构建依赖感知的证据图实现推理过程可溯源，结合原子操作驱动的符号推理与证据约束的神经推理，并引入过程和结果两级评估模块。
