Conclusion: NeSy-CSA能够将发现的关键场景转化为可复用知识，用于后续测试与安全分析。

Abstract: Understanding why discovered scenarios become critical in scenario-based testing is essential for effectively leveraging them in decision-making systems. Reasoning about such criticality can be formulated as an attribution problem. However, across different decision-making tasks, the causes of criticality may involve diverse state variables, interaction patterns, and failure mechanisms, making attribution an inherently open-ended problem beyond predefined explanation spaces. Existing attribution methods still struggle to balance open-ended reasoning flexibility with the interpretability and traceability required for critical scenario reasoning. To address this limitation, we propose NeSy-CSA, a neuro-symbolic framework that transforms open-ended critical scenario attribution from unconstrained explanation generation into structured and traceable reasoning. NeSy-CSA narrows the attribution space by selecting relevant factors, makes the reasoning process traceable through a dependency-aware evidence graph, and executes symbolic reasoning procedures derived from atomic operations, coordinated with evidence-constrained neural inference to support flexible open-ended attribution. We further introduce a process-level and result-level assessment module to evaluate the structural validity of the attribution process and the behavioral effectiveness of the attribution results under controlled interventions. Experiments across four decision-making environments show that NeSy-CSA improves two intervention-based measures of attribution effectiveness by 18.32% and 13.67% over LLM-based baselines. These results demonstrate its potential to transform discovered critical scenarios into reusable knowledge for subsequent testing and safety analysis.

</details>


### [28] [AdaptiveSD A Stability-Aware, Runtime-Adaptive Speculative Decoding Framework with Multi-Policy Orchestration for CPU-Constrained LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03876)
*Sadra Saremi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对GGUF量化模型在CPU推理中因内存带宽限制导致的推测解码性能下降问题，提出了AdaptiveSD框架，通过运行时监控、自适应草案控制器、动态策略引擎和KV缓存协调层四个组件的协同反馈循环，实现鲁棒的推测解码执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小规模GGUF量化模型在设备端推理场景中越来越普及，但纯CPU实现面临严重的内存带宽约束。固定深度的推测解码技术在此场景下常因带宽饱和、不稳定或资源耗尽导致性能下降甚至系统崩溃，亟需一种能够自适应调整的执行框架。

Method: 提出了AdaptiveSD框架，包含四个紧密耦合的组件：(1) 运行时监控引擎，跟踪多个计算相关信号；(2) 自适应草案控制器，实施11条规则策略层级，优先保障系统资源；(3) 动态策略引擎，结合启发式和强化学习技术动态调整策略；(4) KV缓存协调层，通过INT8影子缓冲区和位置感知驱逐实现细粒度缓存管理。

Result: 与传统方法仅关注吞吐量不同，本文从多个指标评估有效性：浪费的草案计算量、令牌间延迟分散度以及推测效率的标准度量。

Conclusion: AdaptiveSD提供了一种全运行时自适应的推测解码框架，确保在不同模型类型和工作负载下都能实现鲁棒可靠的执行，有效解决了内存带宽约束环境下固定深度推测解码的性能退化问题。

Abstract: With the rise of small quantized GGUF-based language models and their increasing use for on-device inference tasks, we have seen the growing need for an approach capable of reliably delivering these models at scale even under severe memory bandwidth constraints such as those imposed by pure CPU implementations. Fixed-depth speculative decoding has emerged as one promising technique, but in practice, it often leads to performance degradation due to either bandwidth saturation, instability, or even catastrophic resource exhaustion resulting in system failure. To overcome this problem, we introduce AdaptiveSD, a fully runtime-adaptive speculative decoding framework aimed at ensuring robust, reliable execution across the spectrum of model types and workloads. Our solution consists of four tightly-coupled components working together in a continuous feedback loop: a Runtime Monitoring Engine tracking multiple signals relevant to ongoing computation, an Adaptive Draft Controller enforcing an eleven rule policy hierarchy prioritizing system resource preservation over raw draft count, a Dynamic Policy Engine employing a suite of heuristic and reinforcement learning techniques to dynamically modify policies depending upon workload behavior, and finally, a KV Cache Coordination Layer managing cache states with fine-grained control through INT8 shadow buffers and position aware evictions. While conventional approaches focus solely on maximizing throughput, we instead assess the effectiveness of our approach based on several key metrics including wasted drafted compute and inter-token latency dispersion alongside standard measures of speculative efficiency.

</details>


### [29] [Knowing When to Stop: Predicting Execution-Consistency Convergence in Text-to-SQL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03991)
*Yaron Anavi,Mor Aisenberg,Nadav Nesher,Elena Khabibullina,Isabella Cattinelli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种轻量级1D模型，用于预测Text-to-SQL管道中多次执行结果一致性的收敛点，从而动态决定何时停止重复调用，在BIRD基准测试和两个生产数据集上表现出色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通过重复调用LLM并观察结果一致性来估计Text-to-SQL的可信度，但何时停止（即一致性何时收敛）是一个未解决的问题，需要高效且自适应的停止策略。

Method: 将问题建模为收敛预测问题，训练轻量级1D模型实时观测运行一致性轨迹，在每个步骤判断继续运行是否可能显著改变结果；同时设计了Beta-Bernoulli停止规则和学习型运行次数基线作为对比，并引入排列顺序作为训练增强手段。

Result: 在BIRD基准测试和两个生产数据集上，该方法能根据每个问题自适应调整停止点，在一致性早收敛时提前停止，晚收敛时继续运行；在注入噪声模拟不完美评判者的情况下仍能可靠预测收敛。

Conclusion: 该方法在不同数据集上性能一致，证明了轻量级收敛预测模型在Text-to-SQL可信度评估中的有效性和鲁棒性，为动态停止策略提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Repeated LLM calls are the standard way to estimate how trustworthy a Text-to-SQL result is: run the pipeline multiple times, judge each SQL execution, and use the consistency of the verdicts as a confidence signal. The open question is when to stop, when the consistency has converged. We formulate this as a convergence-prediction problem and train a family of lightweight 1-D models that observe the running consistency trajectory and decide, at each step, whether further runs are unlikely to shift it materially, and we benchmark them against a principled Beta-Bernoulli stopping rule and a learned run-count baseline. On the BIRD benchmark and two production customer datasets, our method adapts its stopping point to each user question, halting sooner when consistency converges early and continuing longer when it converges late. We further show that the weak serial correlation between runs lets us permute their order as a training augmentation, controlled by a tunable shuffling weight. Performance stays consistent across the three datasets, and to mimic an imperfect production judge we inject noise into the correct/incorrect verdicts obtained by comparing the generated and ground-truth SQL results, showing that the method still predicts convergence reliably.

</details>


### [30] [Dictionaries, Not Darwin: Set-Level Selection Beats LLM Evolution in Scientific Equation Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04108)
*Pan Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文审计了LLM驱动的科学发现迭代循环（生成-选择-反馈）的实际效果，发现该循环与独立随机采样无显著差异。进而提出PTB-Search方法，通过一次生成+集合级稀疏选择实现高效科学发现，在多项基准上大幅超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM驱动的科学发现方法广泛采用迭代进化循环（parent-conditioned evolution），但在有限样本下，结构欠定且插值容易，该循环是否真正有效尚不明确。本文旨在审计该循环的实质贡献，并探索更优的替代范式。

Method: 1) 在科学方程发现任务中，对LLM迭代进化循环进行严格审计，控制LLM调用预算，对比父代条件生成与独立采样；2) 提出PTB-Search：单次独立采样LLM候选，提取可重用项构建问题级字典，在训练集上执行集合级稀疏选择（最小二乘系数）；3) 在多个基准（LLM-SRBench 239问题）和程序域压力测试中评估。

Result: 1) 父代条件进化与独立采样无显著差异（中位OOD NMSE 0.045 vs 0.049），多父代交叉更差，最终成功由初始提议质量决定；2) 集合级选择器解决165-169/717个单元，单术语缩减仅解决74-78个；3) PTB-Search在LLM-SRBench达到73.2% Acc0.1（Llama-3.1-8B）和77.0%（DeepSeek-V4），优于基线49.2%，仅用1/10调用预算。

Conclusion: LLM应被理解为材料供应商，科学发现的真正驱动力是外部集合级选择机制。PTB-Search的一代方法证明了这一范式在成分化科学发现中的有效性，但生成计数仍不可靠，保留外部状态在更困难的非线性空间中有帮助。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used as evolutionary engines for scientific discovery: generate candidates, select winners, feed them back as parents, and repeat. We audit whether this loop actually compounds discovery in scientific equation discovery, a setting where finite samples make structure underdetermined and interpolation easy. Under matched LLM-call budgets, parent-conditioned evolution is indistinguishable from fresh independent sampling: median OOD NMSE is 0.045 vs. 0.049, instructed multi-parent crossover is worse, final success is predicted by initial proposal quality, and multiple iteration schemes fail to add solved problems. Operationally, the loop reduces to what it produces: a dictionary of candidate terms. We turn that diagnosis into PTB-Search, a one-generation method for componentized scientific discovery. PTB-Search samples independent LLM proposals once, extracts reusable terms into a per-problem dictionary, and performs train-only set-level sparse selection with least-squares coefficients. Its central principle is that underdetermined data identifies the joint behavior of term sets, not reliable per-term credit. On identical dictionaries and zero additional LLM calls, set-level selectors solve 165--169 of 717 cells, while single-term reductions solve only 74--78. On the official 239-problem LLM-SRBench split, PTB-Search reaches 73.2% Acc0.1 with Llama-3.1-8B and 77.0% with a single-seed DeepSeek-V4 anchor, versus 49.2% for the best reported baseline, using one tenth of the standardized call budget. A program-domain stress test gives a scoped boundary: generation count remains unreliable, while retained external state can help in harder non-linear spaces. Across these results, LLMs are best understood as material suppliers; discovery is carried by external set-level selection over reusable components.

</details>


### [31] [CertMix: Certified, Data-Efficient Metamaterial Design by Affine Mixing of Aligned Neural-Implicit Weight Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04123)
*Yifan Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CertMix是一种数据高效的力学超材料逆设计框架，利用对齐的周期性神经隐式场表示，通过约束仿射混合和可微分均匀化实现高精度、可外推且有统计保证的设计，仅需50个样本即可达到10⁻⁴的标量属性误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于学习的力学超材料逆设计方法数据需求大、多为插值性、且无法保证生成设计满足目标规格，亟需一种数据高效、可外推并提供统计保证的设计框架。

Method: 将每个样本单元表示为小型周期性神经隐式场（SIREN有符号距离解码器），通过对齐的权重向量空间使混合系数与均匀化弹性张量近似线性；将目标设计转化为约束仿射混合问题，通过可微分周期性均匀化器求解；利用负系数实现外推，线性不匹配信任区域保证混合有效性，分裂共形校准将不匹配信号转化为分布无关的误差保证。

Result: 仅需50个样本即可达到10⁻⁴标量属性误差，比基于1000个样本的条件生成基线低2-3个数量级；在样本范围外保持准确；比每目标拓扑优化快57倍且避免棋盘格和封闭空洞；可扩展到空间梯度场、3D三周期曲面及认证的跑鞋中底应用。

Conclusion: CertMix在数据效率、精度、外推能力、速度和可认证性方面均显著优于现有方法，为力学超材料逆设计提供了一种实用且可靠的解决方案。

Abstract: Inverse design of mechanical metamaterials seeks a periodic unit cell whose homogenized elastic properties meet a prescribed target, but current learning-based methods are data-hungry, mostly interpolative, and provide no guarantee that the generated design satisfies the specification. We introduce CertMix, a data-efficient framework that represents each exemplar unit cell as a small periodic neural implicit field, specifically a SIREN signed-distance decoder overfit from a shared anchor, so that exemplar weight vectors become aligned and directly comparable. The key observation is that, in this aligned weight space, the homogenized elasticity tensor is approximately linear in the mixing coefficients. Targeted design therefore reduces to a small constrained affine-mixing problem solved with a differentiable periodic homogenizer in the loop. Negative coefficients enable extrapolation beyond the exemplar range, a linearity-mismatch trust region keeps blends valid, and split-conformal calibration converts the mismatch signal into a distribution-free certificate on achieved-property error. From as few as 50 exemplars, CertMix attains a scaled property error of $10^{-4}$, roughly two to three orders of magnitude below conditional generative baselines trained on 1000 cells. It remains accurate far outside the exemplar range, is $57\times$ faster than per-target topology optimization while avoiding checkerboards and enclosed voids, and extends to spatially graded fields, 3D triply periodic surfaces, and a certified running-shoe midsole application.

</details>


### [32] [Quantize the Target, Quantize the Drafter: Efficient Inference with Qwen3.5-4B](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04244)
*Jaeyeon Kim,Jewon Lee,Bo-Kyeong Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合量化感知蒸馏和块扩散草稿模型的推测解码系统，在NVIDIA A10G GPU上实现Qwen3.5-4B的低延迟推理，相比基线获得6.978倍平均加速，排名第三。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在资源受限的NVIDIA A10G GPU上实现Qwen3.5-4B的低延迟服务，需要克服量化精度损失和推测解码效率两大挑战。

Method: 1) 量化感知蒸馏：在保留原始量化网格的前提下对目标模型进行蒸馏以恢复精度；2) 块扩散草稿模型（block-diffusion drafter）：两阶段训练（先学习高精度目标，后适应低精度目标）；3) 对草稿模型进一步量化并采用滑动窗口注意力以降低开销。

Result: 相比基线获得6.978倍平均加速，同时满足所需质量阈值，竞赛总排名第三。

Conclusion: 本方法验证了量化感知蒸馏与定制化推测解码相结合在资源受限环境下的有效性，为实际LLM推理提供了有益参考。

Abstract: This report describes our approach to the Efficient Qwen Competition, where the goal is to enable low-latency serving of Qwen3.5-4B on a resource-constrained NVIDIA A10G GPU. Our system combines a quantized target model with speculative decoding. To recover accuracy, we apply quantization-aware distillation to the target model while retaining the original quantization grid. To speed up decoding, a block-diffusion drafter specialized for the quantized target model is trained using a two-stage procedure: first learning from the high-precision target and then adapting to the low-precision target. Because the drafter is invoked at every speculative decoding step, we further reduce its overhead with quantization and sliding-window attention, preserving draft-token acceptance while improving long-context decoding latency. As a result, our submission achieves a 6.978$\times$ average speedup over the baseline while satisfying the required quality thresholds, ranking 3rd overall. We hope these results provide useful insights for practical LLM inference. The code and resources are available at https://github.com/nota-github/adaptfm-quant-dflash

</details>


### [33] [HiFA4: Training-Free 4-bit FlashAttention on Ascend HIF4 NPUs for LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04302)
*Hui Dong,Yanzhao Li,Jie Gao,Chunlu Li,Zhiyuan Zhang,Yupeng Sun,Zhenyuan Chen,Zhiqiang Zou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HiFA4 是一种面向昇腾 NPU 的 4-bit 算子级后训练设计，通过 HIF4 Cube GEMM 实现 FlashAttention 中的 QK^T 和 PV 操作，结合 Smooth-QK 和 P-Reordering 两种机制，在保持在线 softmax 状态为 FP16 的前提下，显著降低量化带来的精度损失和决策漂移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在昇腾 NPU 上实现 4-bit 精度的 FlashAttention 推理加速，同时最小化低比特量化导致的模型精度退化。

Method: 1) Smooth-QK：对经过 RoPE 后的 Q 和 K 进行校准静态的逐通道等价重缩放，将量化难点从 K 转移到 Q，避免推理时逐 tile 在线调整。2) P-Reordering：使用与 PV GEMM 中相同的量化注意力权重 P_hat 累积 softmax 归一化因子，而非高精度重建版本，并将该归一化融合到 PV Cube GEMM 中。

Result: 在 Qwen3-8B 上恢复直接 HIF4 量化引入的 37.5% 精度差距，将样本加权准确率损失从 1.12 个百分点降至 0.70 pp，BF16 不一致的 MMLU 预测从 16.3% 降至 8.2%，MMLU 准确率退化减少 57%（从 1071 降至 465）。Gemma2-9B 上保持在 BF16 的 0.7 pp 以内。其他模型上 P-Reordering 使 MMLU 退化减少 41-52%。指令调度分析预计相较 BF16 可实现 35.4% 的关键路径延迟缩减。

Conclusion: HiFA4 是首个面向昇腾 HIF4 的 FlashAttention 4-bit 算子级设计，通过 Smooth-QK 和 P-Reordering 有效缓解低比特量化的精度损失，并具备显著的延迟优化潜力。

Abstract: We present HiFA4, a post-training operator-level design that executes both QK^T and PV in FlashAttention as 4-bit HIF4 Cube GEMMs for LLM inference on Ascend NPUs, while maintaining the online softmax state in FP16. To our knowledge, HiFA4 is the first Ascend-HIF4-targeted design of this kind evaluated on standard NLP benchmarks.
  HiFA4 combines two mechanisms. Smooth-QK applies a calibration-static per-channel equivalent rescaling to Q and K after RoPE, transferring quantization difficulty from K to Q without per-tile online reduction at inference. P-Reordering accumulates the softmax normalizer from the same quantized attention weights P_hat used in the PV GEMM, rather than from a higher-precision reconstruction. We show that this inconsistent formulation introduces a coherent output-scaling error, and validate the effect on a Qwen3-8B Layer-0 MMLU trace, where all 3.6M measured attention tiles exhibit net probability-mass loss with median epsilon_bar = -0.064. P-Reordering also allows the normalizer to be fused into the PV Cube GEMM.
  Across five LLMs, HiFA4 reduces quantization-induced decision drift. On Qwen3-8B, it recovers 37.5% of the accuracy gap introduced by direct HIF4 quantization, narrows the sample-weighted accuracy loss from 1.12 pp to 0.70 pp, reduces BF16-inconsistent MMLU predictions from 16.3% to 8.2%, and cuts MMLU accuracy regressions by 57% (1071 to 465). On Gemma2-9B, mild smoothing keeps HiFA4 within 0.7 pp of BF16 while reducing MMLU regressions by 27%. On LLaMA3.1-8B, Mistral-7B, and Phi-4B, where Smooth-QK is disabled, P-Reordering with the adopted Q-Mean auxiliary still reduces full-set MMLU regressions by 41-52%. A preliminary instruction-scheduling analysis projects a 35.4% critical-path latency reduction relative to BF16 by fusing the softmax normalizer into the PV Cube GEMM; on-hardware validation is left to future work.

</details>


### [34] [On the effectiveness of reward functions in reinforcement learning for confidence calibration of large language models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04332)
*Chee Heng Tan,Zhuoyi Lin,Mehul Motani,Wee Sun Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了使用强化学习同时提升大语言模型推理准确性和置信度表达能力时，可能出现的"置信度奖励黑客"现象，提出了不可黑客置信度奖励方案的概念及其谱系，并建议将奖励方案作为超参数以优化准确率-校准度的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在强化学习训练LLM的过程中，同时优化推理准确性和置信度表达时，设计不当的奖励方案可能导致模型有意答错以便能自信地声称自己错误（置信度奖励黑客），需要解决这一奖励设计问题。

Method: 设计了两种奖励函数分别对应正确和错误时表达置信度的情况；提出了不可黑客置信度奖励方案的概念及其谱系定义；通过实验验证了选择性置信度奖励黑客在现实数据集中的发生情况。

Result: 证明了奖励方案若非不可黑客设计，在实际数据集中会发生选择性置信度奖励黑客；最佳校准-准确率权衡的奖励方案取决于数据集和应用场景。

Conclusion: 建议将奖励方案作为超参数，根据应用需求优化准确率与校准度的权衡。

Abstract: In this paper, we consider the setting where large language models (LLMs) are trained using reinforcement learning (RL) to simultaneously improve reasoning accuracy and verbalize its confidence. Our reward scheme uses two functions for rewarding confidence verbalized by the LLM: one when the LLM is correct and a different one when the LLM is incorrect. With a poorly designed reward scheme, the LLM may be incentivized to answer incorrectly so that it can be confident that its answer is indeed incorrect, a phenomenon that we call confidence reward hacking. We propose the concept of non-hackable confidence reward schemes and define a spectrum of such reward schemes for RL confidence calibration training in LLMs. We demonstrate that selective confidence reward hacking can occur in practical datasets with reward schemes that are not designed to be non-hackable. We also demonstrate that the reward scheme with the best calibration to accuracy tradeoff depends on the dataset and the application, and propose using the reward scheme as a hyperparameter to optimize the tradeoffs in accordance to what is important for the application. The code of our experiments is available in https://anonymous.4open.science/r/rl-confidence-calibration-9ED4/README.md.

</details>


### [35] [How Many Initial Points Does Bayesian Optimization Need?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04356)
*Mujin Cheon,James Odgers,Dong-Yeun Koh,Calvin Tsay*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 贝叶斯优化的初始随机评估点数n0存在U型成本曲线（过少或过多都会浪费资源），Thompson Sampling对n0不敏感但性能略低，建议使用多步前瞻BO或适量增大n0。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 贝叶斯优化中初始随机评估点数n0的选择目前依赖启发式方法，缺乏系统研究，本文旨在揭示n0对优化总成本的影响规律。

Method: 通过大量实验对比不同n0值下（MLE、贝叶斯MCMC、精确GP超参数及多种采集函数）的总成本（随机初始点+BO迭代次数），并使用3D BO轨迹可视化分析U型现象的成因。

Result: 总成本关于n0呈U型曲线；Thompson Sampling的简单遗憾和总成本对n0不敏感但整体偏高；U型现象源于方差驱动BO的边界问题（前期在超立方体角落浪费预算）。

Conclusion: 推荐使用多步前瞻BO；若无法调优n0则使用Thompson Sampling；可调优时选择较大的n0值。

Abstract: Bayesian Optimization (BO) generally begins with an initialization phase: a batch of $n_0$ uninformed evaluations. The choice of $n_0$ remains largely heuristic, and we empirically observe that the total cost (random initial points plus BO iterations needed to find the global optimum) is U-shaped in $n_0$, i.e., a practitioner wastes resources by selecting either too low or too high a value of $n_0$. We find this tradeoff persists across MLE, Bayesian MCMC, and exact GP hyperparameters, as well as across acquisition functions. Toward the latter, Thompson Sampling appears an exception, with both total cost and simple regret essentially $n_0$-agnostic, though higher in our experiments. We attribute this U-shape to the known boundary issue of variance-driven BO: BO burns early budget on corners of the hypercube before turning inward. We demonstrate this effect using a 3D BO trajectory where the exact hyperparameters are known. We conclude with practical recommendations: use multi-step lookahead BO where possible; otherwise use Thompson Sampling when $n_0$ cannot be tuned, and a generously large $n_0$ when it can.

</details>


### [36] [NKI-Agent: Domain-Specific Fine-Tuning and Agentic Tool Use for Neuron Kernel Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04395)
*Junjie Tang,Jun Huan,Hao Zhou,Yuhao Zhang,Lin Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: NKI-Agent是首个针对AWS Neuron芯片（Trainium/Inferentia）的自动化NKI内核生成系统，结合领域特定监督微调（SFT）与编译-验证-修复智能体循环，在Trn1硬件上达到77.3%通过率，并开源NKIBench基准测试集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM内核生成方法在CUDA上表现优异，但针对AWS Trainium/Inferentia等新兴AI加速器的自动化内核生成与优化尚未被解决。NKI编程面临多引擎架构、基于tile的编程、显式数据移动等多层挑战，且缺乏公开的训练数据、基准测试和工具增强型智能体。

Method: 1) 将CUDA-Agent框架适配至Neuron硬件；2) 构建6,000个NKI内核生成任务的训练集及250个任务的NKIBench基准测试；3) 采用领域特定监督微调（SFT）结合编译-验证-修复智能体循环；4) 评估SFT与GRPO（组相对策略优化）两种训练策略。

Result: Claude Opus 4.8配合rank-aware系统提示在NKIBench上达77.3%通过率；无智能体工具时单次生成仅6%；SFT训练的Qwen3-Coder-30B-A3B以1/100成本达25.0%通过率，超越Claude Sonnet 4（15.0%）；GRPO+二元编译奖励未能提升SFT效果。

Conclusion: SFT结合智能体循环是NKI内核生成的有效范式，工具使用至关重要；GRPO需更精细的奖励设计才能发挥优势；NKI-Agent填补了Neuron芯片自动化内核生成的空白。

Abstract: Recent agentic approaches to LLM-based kernel generation have achieved impressive results on CUDA. For emerging AI accelerators such as AWS Trainium and Inferentia, automated kernel generation and optimization remain largely unaddressed. Writing kernels for these chips via the Neuron Kernel Interface (NKI) is particularly challenging: developers must navigate a multi-engine architecture, tile-based programming, and explicit data movement across multi-level memory hierarchy. Moreover, no publicly-available training data, benchmarks, or tool-augmented agents exist for this domain. We introduce NKI-Agent, the first system combining domain-specific supervised fine-tuning (SFT) with a compile-verify-fix agent loop for NKI kernel generation. We adapt the existing CUDA-Agent framework to Neuron hardware, curate 6,000 NKI kernel generation tasks for training, and construct NKIBench, a 250-task benchmark across three difficulty levels. Evaluated on real Trn1 hardware, NKI-Agent with Claude Opus 4.8 and a rank-aware system prompt achieves a 77.3% pass rate on the 150-task NKIBench. We show that tool use is critical: Opus 4.8 scores 6% in single-shot mode without agent tools. On a 60-task subset, we show that an SFT-trained Qwen3-Coder-30B-A3B achieves 25.0% pass rate at 1/100th the cost, outperforming Claude Sonnet 4 (15.0%). We also report that Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) with binary compilation reward fails to improve over SFT, providing guidance on reward design for RL-based kernel generation.

</details>


### [37] [Full-Stack FP4: Stable LLM Pretraining with Quantized Projections, Optimizers, and Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04422)
*Siyu Ding,Mingchuan Ma,Jiabo Tong,Xingrun Xing,Ziming Wang,Guoqi Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Full-Stack FP4，首个完整的NVFP4预训练框架，通过模块级精度策略（线性层的LoRA-SVD分解、优化器状态变换、注意力混合精度）解决了全栈4比特预训练中线性层、优化器状态和注意力模块的数值稳定性瓶颈，在3B/64B-token预训练中实现接近BF16的性能（仅1.47%损失差距）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有NVFP4预训练方法主要针对Transformer线性层，忽略了优化器状态、优化器算法和注意力模块在4比特流水线中的稳定性问题，导致无法实现稳定的全栈4比特预训练。这三个核心模块存在独特的数值失效模式：线性层存在量化噪声极限和维度传播误差放大；AdamW二阶矩是重尾非负值，对低精度分母敏感；注意力存在错误传播路径，需要严格的前反向量化一致性。

Method: 1) 线性层：LoRA-SVD轻量级分解抑制量化噪声，突破直接量化误差上限；2) 优化器：设计AdamW二阶矩变换实现稳健NVFP4存储，支持原生NVFP4 Newton-Schulz迭代用于Root (Muon)优化器；3) 注意力：混合精度方案量化Q/K/V和反向dS，保护BF16中的脆弱路径，配合统一张量复用维持前反向一致性；4) 分析低比特矩阵乘法的快速误差累积以及PV/dOV^T注意力分支的极端敏感性。

Result: 线性层损失差距从1.40%缩小到0.61%；3B/64B-token预训练实现接近BF16性能，仅1.47%损失差距。

Conclusion: Full-Stack FP4验证了可行的稳定端到端NVFP4 LLM预训练，所有模块即插即用，具有累积稳定性和效率提升。

Abstract: Recent NVFP4 pretraining methods mainly target transformer linear layers, leaving optimizer states, optimizer arithmetic and attention underexplored in 4-bit pipelines. This critical gap blocks stable full-stack 4-bit pretraining, as the three core modules exhibit unique numerical failure patterns: linear layers hit hard quantization noise limits with dimension-propagated error amplification; AdamW second moments are heavy-tailed non-negative values fragile to low-precision denominators; attention carries error-prone computation paths demanding strict forward-backward quantization consistency. We propose Full-Stack FP4, the first complete NVFP4 pretraining framework resolving all three stability bottlenecks via module-wise precision strategies. For linear projections, LoRA-SVD lightweight decomposition suppresses quantization noise and breaks the direct-quantization error ceiling, shrinking the linear-only loss gap from 1.40% to 0.61%. For optimizers, we design AdamW second-moment transformation for robust NVFP4 storage and fully support native NVFP4 Newton-Schulz iterations for the Root (Muon) optimizer. For attention, a mixed-precision scheme quantizes Q/K/V and backward dS while guarding vulnerable paths in BF16, paired with unified tensor reuse to sustain forward-backward alignment. We further analyze fast error accumulation in naive low-bit matrix multiplication and the extreme sensitivity of PV / dOV^T attention branches. All modules are plug-and-play with cumulative stability and efficiency improvements. Our 3B/64B-token pretraining validates near-BF16 performance with merely 1.47% loss gap, verifying feasible stable end-to-end NVFP4 LLM pretraining.

</details>


### [38] [Regime-Conditional Stabilisation of LLM-Augmented Cooperative Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04470)
*Faid Keddouri,Sohaib Houhou,Aissa Boulmerka,Nadir Farhi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了LLM生成的奖励权重在离策略多智能体强化学习中动态更新会破坏势能基奖励塑造的平稳性假设并污染经验回放缓冲区，提出相位冻结调度和指数移动平均两种稳定策略，通过三个环境的实验建立了三机制分类体系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM为MARL提供了自然的奖励塑造接口，但训练过程中LLM奖励权重的动态更新对算法稳定性的影响尚未被系统研究。

Method: 理论分析证明动态LLM奖励权重违反PBRS平稳性假设并污染经验回放缓冲区；提出基于相位的冻结调度（Phase-Based Freeze Schedule）和指数移动平均平滑（EMA）两种稳定策略；在三个合作环境（Simple Spread、Level-Based Foraging、SMAC 3m）中使用QMIX算法及VDN扩展进行实验验证。

Result: 建立了三机制分类：增强机制（基础策略有效，EMA提升12.3个百分点）、必需机制（基础策略无效，任何塑造都能解锁任务）、补充机制（基础策略接近饱和，稳定塑造无增益，不稳定塑造增加方差）。

Conclusion: 奖励信号平稳性是LLM-MARL集成中的必要设计约束，机制位置是预测动态LLM塑造是否有害的实用指标。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) offer a natural interface for translating human objectives into reward signals for cooperative multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL), yet the training-time dynamics of this integration remain poorly understood. We show that dynamically updating LLM-generated reward weights during off-policy MARL violates the stationarity assumption of Potential-Based Reward Shaping (PBRS) and contaminates the experience replay buffer, whose stored transitions carry reward labels computed under stale shaping weights. We characterise the result as a regime-dependent failure whose severity depends on how competent the unshaped baseline already is. To control it we propose two stabilisation strategies: a Phase-Based Freeze Schedule that enforces strict stationarity within training phases, and Exponential Moving Average (EMA) smoothing that bounds per-episode weight drift. We evaluate across three cooperative environments and five random seeds with QMIX, complemented by an exploratory VDN extension, yielding a three-regime taxonomy. In the augmentative regime (Simple Spread), where the baseline is functional (74.4 %), EMA significantly improves success to 86.7 % ($+12.3$ pp, $p<0.01$) while naive dynamic updates collapse it to 15.2 %. In the essential regime (Level-Based Foraging), where the baseline is broken (0.1 %), any shaping unlocks the task (95.9 % under EMA). In the supplementary regime (SMAC 3m), where the baseline is near-saturated (98.8 %), stabilised shaping preserves performance (99.9 %) while unstabilised shaping adds variance without gain. These findings establish reward-signal stationarity as a necessary design constraint and indicate that regime placement is a practical predictor of whether dynamic LLM shaping helps or harms.

</details>


### [39] [Auto: The AGI Compiler](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04542)
*Jaber Jaber,Osama Jaber*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Auto编译器，将LLM代理的运行时行为编译为经过验证的WebAssembly二进制文件，通过分层运行时和守卫机制实现确定性行为的自动化提取与复用，在保持96.9%一致性的同时将边际成本从59降至2微美元。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM代理每次运行时都从头开始逐token推导行为，导致高延迟和高成本，且无法积累和复用已验证的确定性技能。

Method: Auto编译器记录代理运行时行为，测量并提取其中的确定性部分，编译为经过验证的WebAssembly认知二进制文件；采用分层运行时系统，通过校准守卫进行行为验证，守卫失败时回退到参考代理并捕获轨迹重新编译。

Result: 在AUTO-BENCH基准上，87.1%的轨迹为确定性；在300项流式任务中，编译三个工件世代后将边际成本从59降至2微美元（端到端6.4倍），在已见证输入上达到96.9%一致性和零错误。

Conclusion: 校准和参考代理的保真度是决定低成本行为能否保持正确的关键因素，而非模型能力本身；系统以可测试的方式实现了将新体验转化为永久、可验证、近乎免费的技能的"AGI编译器"概念。

Abstract: Every LLM agent run re-derives its behavior token by token on a frontier model: brilliant, expensive, slow, and unbounded. We present Auto, a compiler that records live agent behavior, measures which parts are secretly deterministic, extracts them into verified programs or distilled specialists, and emits cognition binaries: WebAssembly artifacts whose manifests carry measured guarantees and whose declared capabilities are physically enforced by the sandbox. A tiered runtime executes compiled behavior behind conformally calibrated guards; guard trips deopt to the reference agent, and the captured trace recompiles back down, so nothing is figured out twice. We use "AGI compiler" in one narrow, testable sense: a system that autonomously converts novel experience into permanent, verified, near-free skill while measuring what it does not know. On AUTO-BENCH, a benchmark we introduce and pre-register, 87.1% of 560 recorded frontier-agent spans are witnessed-deterministic (three of the four censused task families measure 100.0%). On a 300-item stream with three scheduled distribution shifts, the closed loop compiles three artifact generations and drives marginal cost from 59 to 2 micro-dollars per item (6.4x end-to-end) at 96.9% parity on witnessed inputs with zero errors. The same stream also quantifies the failure modes: a loose guard silently mislabels 48.9% of compiled answers, and an unfaithful deopt reference causes the verification gate to refuse recompilation. Calibration and reference fidelity, not model capability, decide whether cheap stays correct. Code: https://github.com/RightNow-AI/auto

</details>


### [40] [A Few Teacher Steps Go a Long Way: Cost-Efficient On-Policy Data Augmentation for Agent Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04574)
*Junze Ye,Jiayi Cheng,Miao Lu,Michal Mankowski,Jose Blanchet,Mohsen Bayati*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将有监督微调中的在线数据构建视为预算分配问题，发现在匹配预算下，在学习者诱导的上下文处放置少量未过滤的教师步骤，比纯行为克隆、更长或更精心筛选的教师完成更高效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决行为克隆中训练时教师诱导上下文与测试时学生诱导上下文不匹配的问题，并系统探索在有限监督资源下如何最优分配教师输出。

Method: 将在线数据构建形式化为预算分配问题，通过 rollout 策略、切换时间分布、延续视界、过滤规则以及两类成本（过滤前的教师推理和SFT保留的教师监督）来系统化设计空间，并在HotpotQA、ALFWorld、Terminal-Bench-Dev上进行比较实验。

Result: 在匹配预算下，学习者诱导上下文处的有界未过滤教师延续优于纯行为克隆；在HotpotQA和ALFWorld上，少步延续达到或超过成功过滤和关键上下文过滤的替代方案。

Conclusion: 少量教师步骤放置在学习者诱导上下文处是比更长或更精心筛选的教师完成更成本高效的监督分配策略。

Abstract: For LLM agents, supervised fine-tuning is not only about teacher labels' quality, but also about which interaction contexts those labels condition on. Pure behavioral cloning uses full teacher demonstrations, creating a mismatch between teacher-induced contexts seen in training and student-induced contexts encountered at test time. Recent work addresses this mismatch by querying a teacher at contexts reached by the student, often with increasingly elaborate filtering of the teacher's continuations. We instead frame on-policy data construction as a budget-allocation problem: under matched supervision resources, should teacher output be spent on more start-to-finish demos, longer continuations, outcome filtering, or broader coverage of learner-induced contexts? We formalize this design space through the rollout policy, switch-time distribution, continuation horizon, filtering rules, and two complementary costs: teacher inference generated before filtering and teacher supervision retained for SFT. Across HotpotQA, ALFWorld, and Terminal-Bench-Dev, bounded unfiltered teacher continuations at learner-induced contexts improve over pure behavioral cloning at matched budgets. On HotpotQA and ALFWorld, where we run the full comparison, few-step continuations match or exceed success-filtered and critical-context-filtered alternatives. Our findings suggest that a few teacher steps, placed at learner-induced contexts, can be a more cost-efficient supervision allocation than longer or more heavily curated teacher completions.

</details>


### [41] [RSPO: Reward-Swap Policy Optimization for Multi-Turn LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04713)
*Qiang Liu,Taian Guo,Ruizhi Qiao,Xing Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Reward-Swap Policy Optimization (RSPO)，通过奖励交换机制结合稠密过程奖励与稀疏结果奖励，解决了长程多轮任务中结果奖励信号稀疏导致收敛慢、以及过程奖励与真实目标不一致的问题，在WebShop和ALFWorld基准上显著提升了GRPO、PPO、GiGPO等算法的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程多轮交互任务中，(1) 稀疏结果奖励导致训练收敛缓慢且无法学习未采样轨迹；(2) 稠密过程奖励虽加速收敛但可能与真实结果奖励存在偏差，误导训练方向。需要一种既能利用过程奖励丰富信息、又能保证与真实结果奖励一致性的方法。

Method: 提出Reward-Swap Policy Optimization (RSPO)，核心是奖励交换机制：在训练过程中交替或动态切换使用过程奖励和结果奖励，利用过程奖励的稠密信号引导探索、保证轨迹多样性，同时通过结果奖励确保优化目标与真实目标一致，避免偏差累积。

Result: 在WebShop和ALFWorld两个具身agent基准上，将RSPO应用于GRPO、PPO、GiGPO等多种强化学习算法，均取得一致的性能提升，证明了方法的有效性和泛化性。

Conclusion: RSPO有效融合了过程奖励的加速收敛优势与结果奖励的目标一致性优势，提升了模型在多轮交互任务中的性能上限，是一种通用的奖励优化框架。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning holds significant potential for training large language models (LLMs) to handle multi-turn interactive tasks. However, in long-horizon, multi-turn tasks characterized by sparse outcome rewards, directly training with outcome rewards often results in slow convergence due to the sparsity of signals and the lack of fine-grained feedback. Furthermore, the model may fail to learn successful trajectories that are not sampled during training, thereby limiting its performance. Conversely, while employing customized dense process rewards provides richer signals and accelerates convergence, these surrogate rewards may exhibit potential misalignment with the ground-truth outcome rewards. This inconsistency can bias the training direction and ultimately degrade the model's final performance. In this work, we propose Reward-Swap Policy Optimization (RSPO), a method designed to leverage the rich information from dense process rewards to facilitate training with outcome rewards. By utilizing a reward-swap mechanism, RSPO ensures the diversity of sampled trajectories while guaranteeing consistency between the optimization objective and the true outcome rewards, thereby elevating the performance ceiling of the model. We conduct extensive experiments on two challenging agent benchmarks, WebShop and ALFWorld. By applying our method to various reinforcement learning algorithms, including GRPO, PPO, and GiGPO, we demonstrate that RSPO achieves consistent performance improvements across different baselines and benchmarks.

</details>


### [42] [Multi-Turn On-Policy Distillation with Prefix Replay](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04763)
*Baohao Liao,Hanze Dong,Christof Monz,Xinxing Xu,Li Dong,Furu Wei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ReOPD方法，通过复用预收集的教师轨迹作为重放前缀，在无需环境交互的情况下实现多轮智能体任务的在线策略蒸馏，解决"前缀陷阱"问题，训练速度提升至少4倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 完全在线的策略蒸馏（OPD）在多轮智能体任务中成本极高，因为每次更新都需要学生模型重新与环境交互并查询教师模型。需要一种更高效的替代方案。

Method: 提出ReOPD（Replayed-Prefix On-Policy Distillation），使用预收集的教师轨迹作为重放前缀，学生在选定步骤执行动作，教师提供密集的逐步骤监督而不进行新的环境交互。引入步衰减采样策略，优先采样早期、偏移较小的前缀，解决"前缀陷阱"问题。

Result: 在数学推理和搜索环境等多个任务上，ReOPD保持或提升了OPD级别的准确率，训练期间零工具调用，每训练步速度至少比OPD快4倍。

Conclusion: ReOPD将昂贵的智能体-环境交互转化为可重用的离线资源，实现了跨工具、任务和环境的高可扩展性蒸馏。

Abstract: We study on-policy distillation (OPD) for agentic tasks, where an LLM agent interacts with an environment over multiple turns and a student imitates a teacher over these multi-turn interaction histories. Fully online OPD is costly because each update requires fresh student rollouts through the environment and teacher queries at visited histories. We propose Replayed-Prefix On-Policy Distillation (ReOPD), an off-environment alternative that reuses pre-collected teacher trajectories as replayed prefixes: the student acts at selected steps, while the teacher provides dense per-step supervision without executing new environment interactions. We show that multi-turn OPD introduces a prefix trap: making histories more student-on-policy improves relevance to the student, but can query the teacher on histories where its target is unreliable. This creates a two-sided distribution shift between student occupancy and teacher reliability. ReOPD addresses this by treating multi-turn OPD as a reliability-aware prefix distribution design and implements it with a simple step-decaying sampling schedule that emphasizes early, lower-shift prefixes. Across mathematical reasoning with Python and search environments over multiple teacher and student model scales, ReOPD preserves or improves OPD-level accuracy, uses zero tool calls during student training, and is at least 4$\times$ faster per training step than OPD. ReOPD therefore turns expensive agent-environment interaction into a reusable offline resource, enabling scalable distillation across tools, tasks, and environments.

</details>


### [43] [Active Learning on Adversarially Corrupted Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04869)
*Marco Bressan,Nicolò Cesa-Bianchi,Tommaso d`Orsi,Emmanuel Esposito,Silvio Lattanzi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了对抗性图结构下的主动学习问题，其中攻击者通过添加边来隐藏图中的“ corrupted vertices”。作者提出了一种高效算法，通过少量标签查询近似恢复被破坏的顶点，其查询复杂度与攻击者能力及图的顶点扩展性（vertex expansion）多项式相关。该算法基于 sum-of-squares 技术求解带基数约束的最小顶点扩展问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实场景中存在恶意实体篡改网络结构以隐藏自身，现有主动学习算法缺乏对抗性结构攻击下的鲁棒性研究。本文旨在定义此类对抗模型，并探索图连通性度量——顶点扩展性——如何影响主动学习的查询复杂度。

Method: 定义了对抗模型：攻击者可在 corrupted vertices 之间及 corrupted vertices 与原始图 G* 之间添加边，其能力由 corrupted vertices 在 G* 中的邻域大小衡量。提出基于 sum-of-squares 算法求解带基数约束的最小顶点扩展问题，并设计主动学习算法以少量标签查询近似恢复 corrupted vertices。

Result: 得到了一个多项式时间算法，其查询复杂度与攻击者能力及 G* 的顶点扩展性呈多项式关系。实验性结果表明该算法能有效恢复被破坏的顶点子集。

Conclusion: 首次揭示了顶点扩展性在决定对抗性结构攻击下主动学习查询复杂度中的关键作用，为设计鲁棒的主动学习算法提供了新的理论支撑。

Abstract: Motivated by real-world scenarios where malicious entities tamper with existing networks, we define a model where an adversary seeks to hide a set of \emph{corrupted vertices} inside a graph $G^*$. To this end, the adversary can add edges between the corrupted vertices, as well as edges between the corrupted vertices and $G^*$, and its power is then measured by the size of the \emph{neighborhood} of the corrupted vertices in $G^*$. Our goal is to design an active learning algorithm that efficiently finds the subset of corrupted vertices using a small number of label queries. We devise an efficient algorithm that approximately recovers the corrupted vertices with a query complexity that depends polynomially on both the power of the adversary and the \emph{vertex expansion} of $G^*$, a fundamental measure of graph connectivity. At the heart of this result is a polynomial-time algorithm, obtained by carefully adapting sum-of-squares algorithms for approximating minimum expansion, that finds a set with small vertex expansion subject to cardinality constraints. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first time that the vertex expansion is shown to play a key role in determining the query complexity of active learning algorithms robust to structural adversarial attacks.

</details>


### [44] [When Do Foundation Models Pay Off? A Break-Even Analysis of Pretrained Time Series Forecasters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04919)
*Nicholas Tan Jerome,Frank Simon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文首次系统性分析了时间序列基础模型（Chronos、Moirai、Lag-Llama）与经典方法（Naive、ETS、ARIMA、XGBoost）的盈亏平衡点。在30个基准数据集、6种训练集规模下，发现：15个数据集上基础模型无条件优于经典方法；6个数据集上经典方法仅需2%数据即可超越零样本基础模型；9个数据集存在盈亏平衡点（24-8361样本）。提出了无需模型训练的经验规则（n_train<700且季节性强时使用零样本基础模型）和两步决策框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部署时间序列基础模型需要GPU基础设施和工程成本，但无法保证优于XGBoost等经典方法。实践中缺乏系统性的决策依据来判断何时值得投资部署基础模型，需要量化分析盈亏平衡条件。

Method: 在30个基准数据集上，使用6种训练集规模（2%-100%），对比零样本和LoRA微调的基础模型（Chronos、Moirai、Lag-Llama）与经典基线（Naive、ETS、ARIMA、XGBoost）的性能，进行盈亏平衡分析。

Result: 15/30数据集上基础模型在所有训练规模下均优于经典方法；6/30数据集上经典方法仅需2%数据即可超越零样本基础模型；9/30数据集存在盈亏平衡点（24-8361样本）。发现n_train<700且季节性强时零样本基础模型最优，LoRA微调在短序列上可能降低性能。

Conclusion: 提出了两步决策框架：先计算数据集长度和季节性强度，必要时仅运行5-10%的试点实验，帮助实践者在投入完整基础设施前做出基础模型vs经典方法的选择。可靠的自适应预测在该基准规模下仍是一个开放问题。

Abstract: Deploying a time series foundation model requires GPU infrastructure, engineering overhead, and carries no guarantee of improvement over XGBoost. We provide the first systematic break-even analysis answering when this investment pays off. Across 30 benchmark datasets, we compare zero-shot and LoRA fine-tuned foundation models (Chronos, Moirai, Lag-Llama) against classical baselines (Naive, ETS, ARIMA, XGBoost) at six training set sizes from 2% to 100% of available data. Foundation models outperform classical methods at every evaluated training fraction on 15 of 30 datasets -- GPU deployment is unconditionally justified on these regardless of data volume. On 6 datasets, classical methods surpass zero-shot foundation models with as little as 2% of training data (21-2,768 samples); on the remaining 9, break-even ranges from 24 to 8,361 samples. One robust deployment rule requires no model training: if n_train < 700 and seasonality is non-negligible, use FM zero-shot and skip fine-tuning -- this resolves 10 of 30 deployment decisions immediately. Contrary to common practice, LoRA fine-tuning can actively degrade performance on short series. We operationalise these findings as a two-step decision framework -- compute dataset length and seasonality strength, run a brief 5-10% pilot only if needed -- enabling practitioners to make the FM-versus-classical decision before committing to full infrastructure. Four dataset features motivate mechanistic hypotheses for the remaining cases, though reliable automated prediction at this benchmark scale remains an open problem. Code, benchmark, and decision tools are available at https://github.com/nicolaisi/fm-breakeven.

</details>


### [45] [Train Smarter, Not Longer: Memorization-Guided Data Reuse for Efficient LLM Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04969)
*Jingwei Zuo,Cong Zeng,Ilyas Chahed,Maksim Velikanov,Dhia Eddine Rhaiem,Pasquale Balsebre,Abhay Kumar,Younes Belkada,Hakim Hacid*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于“记忆窗口”信号的自适应数据重利用训练范式，通过损失保持动态和评估分数观察模型记忆行为，发现模型性能在远超当前惯例（如四轮上限）的重复训练中仍可持续提升，为有限高质量数据下的高效训练提供理论基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型训练从单次训练转向多轮训练，虽然合理复用高质量数据能提升性能和样本效率，但过度重复会导致过拟合和收益递减。如何确定何时以及如何有效复用数据是一个未被充分探索的关键问题。

Method: 通过观察模型在损失保持动态和下游评估分数中产生的“记忆窗口”信号，提出“记忆引导的数据复用”训练范式，自适应地决定数据复用的时机和方式，包括训练轮数和数据重放调度。

Result: 初步实验发现存在一致的记忆驱动机制：在远超当前常见实践（如四轮上限）的重复训练中，模型性能仍持续提升。

Conclusion: 这些发现为构建记忆感知的训练调度奠定了基础，有助于确定复用预算并推动在有限高质量数据下实现更智能而非更长时间的训练范式。完整的调度器留待未来工作。

Abstract: The training paradigm of large language models has shifted from traditional one-pass training to multi-epoch training, as reasonable reuse of limited high-quality data can improve both model performance and sample efficiency. Meanwhile, excessive repetition introduces the risk of overfitting and diminishing returns. Determining when and how to reuse data effectively thus emerges as a natural but under-explored question. Through a novel observation of model's "Memorization Window" signals derived from loss retention dynamics and downstream evaluation scores, we propose "Memorization-guided Data Reuse", a training paradigm that adaptively determines when and how data should be reused, enabling principled decisions on the number of training epochs and the scheduling of data replays. Our preliminary experiments reveal a consistent memorization-driven regime: performance continues to improve with repetition far beyond current practice (e.g., the commonly cited four-epoch limit). While a full scheduler remains future work, these insights provide a foundation for memorization-aware training schedules, helping to determine reuse budgets and move toward training LLMs smarter rather than longer with limited high-quality data.

</details>


### [46] [SMART: A Machine Learning and Monte Carlo Framework for Rapid Analysis of Stochastic Transistor Aging and Process Variation in Digital Circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05187)
*Arash Esshaghi,Siavash Es'haghi,Gholamreza Shahabadi,Alireza Moradi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对CMOS深纳米工艺下BTI和工艺变化导致的可靠性分析瓶颈，提出SMART框架，利用随机森林回归和贝叶斯优化实现快速高保真度可靠性分析，在ISCAS85基准测试中分析时间减少94.54%，平均精度误差仅1.63%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于密集仿真或大量查找表的可靠性分析方法在大规模设计中扩展性差，成为设计空间探索的关键瓶颈。

Method: 将机器学习与蒙特卡洛仿真相结合，采用随机森林回归直接预测门延迟分布，避免耗时的原子模型参数提取；利用贝叶斯优化自动调参以确保跨库的预测鲁棒性。

Result: 在ISCAS85基准电路上，分析时间较现有方法降低94.54%，平均精度误差仅1.63%。

Conclusion: 通过将计算复杂度转移到离线训练阶段，该框架为设计弹性、可靠性感知的数字系统提供了可扩展、高精度的解决方案。

Abstract: As CMOS technology scales into the deep nanometer regime, digital circuit reliability is increasingly threatened by the combined stochastic effects of Bias Temperature Instability (BTI) and Process Variation (PV). Traditional reliability analysis methods, which rely on computationally intensive simulations or extensive lookup tables, fail to scale efficiently for large designs, creating a critical bottleneck in design space exploration. To address this, we propose SMART, a novel framework that integrates Machine Learning (ML) with Monte Carlo simulation to enable rapid, high-fidelity reliability analysis. SMART employs Random Forest regression to predict gate delay distributions directly, bypassing time-consuming atomic model parameter extractions. Crucially, the model utilizes Bayesian Optimization for automated hyperparameter tuning, ensuring maximum predictive robustness across diverse libraries. Experimental validation on ISCAS85 benchmark circuits demonstrates that SMART achieves a 94.54% reduction in analysis time compared to state-of-the-art methods, while maintaining a remarkable average accuracy error of just 1.63%. By shifting computational complexity to an offline training phase, the proposed framework offers a scalable, accurate solution for designing resilient, reliability-aware digital systems.

</details>


### [47] [CompactionRL: Reinforcement Learning with Context Compaction for Long-Horizon Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05378)
*Yujiang Li,Zhenyu Hou,Yi Jing,Jie Tang,Yuxiao Dong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CompactionRL，一种通过上下文压缩增强强化学习训练长程智能体LLM的策略，联合优化任务执行与摘要生成，在SWE-bench Verified和Terminal-Bench 2.0上显著提升Pass@1指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程任务的交互轨迹容易超过LLM的上下文窗口限制，上下文压缩虽能解决该问题，但如何将其融入强化学习训练尚未被充分探索。

Method: 提出CompactionRL框架，联合优化任务执行与摘要生成，采用token级损失归一化和跨轨迹广义优势估计（GAE），使LLM智能体能够从压缩后的长程轨迹中学习。

Result: 在GLM-4.5-Air（106B-A30B）上，SWE-bench Verified Pass@1达66.8%（+7.0），Terminal-Bench 2.0达24.5%（+3.1）；在GLM-4.7-Flash（30B-A3B）上分别提升5.5和6.8个百分点；已部署于GLM-5.2（750B-A40B）的RL训练管线中。

Conclusion: CompactionRL能有效提升长程智能体LLM的代码任务性能，且已成功应用于更大规模模型的强化学习训练流程。

Abstract: Long-horizon agentic LLMs are increasingly limited by finite context windows, as extended interaction trajectories can exceed the maximum context length before a task is completed. Context compaction offers a natural solution by summarizing previous interaction states and continuing the rollout under a compressed context, but incorporating compaction into reinforcement learning remains underexplored. We propose CompactionRL, a reinforcement learning strategy to train long-horizon agentic LLMs with context compaction. Our approach jointly optimizes task execution and summary generation with token-level loss normalization and cross-trajectory generalized advantage estimation. This design enables the LLM agents to learn from compacted long-horizon trajectories. We train CompactionRL on top of open models and observe consistent performance gains on agentic coding tasks. CompactionRL enables the open GLM-4.5-Air model (106B-A30B) to achieve Pass@1 scores of 66.8% on SWE-bench Verified and 24.5% on Terminal-Bench 2.0, with absolute gains of 7.0 and 3.1 points, respectively. Built upon GLM-4.7-Flash (30B-A3B), CompactionRL improves Pass@1 by 5.5 and 6.8 points, reaching 56.0% on SWE-bench Verified and 20.2% on Terminal-Bench 2.0, respectively. CompactionRL is thus deployed in the RL pipeline for training the open GLM-5.2 model (750B-A40B).

</details>

### [48] [The Objective Decides: When a Learned Dynamics Model Uses a Conserved Quantity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03728)
*Chih-Ting Liao,Xin Cao*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 本文证明，从学习到的动力学模型激活中线性解码守恒量（如能量）并不代表模型实际使用了该量。通过激活干预实验发现，这些量在预测中因果惰性（τ≈0），只有当训练目标奖励该不变量时才变为因果承载（τ→+1）。部署取决于目标而非表征，且存在代数谓词决定何时部署。部署差距能预测OOD准确率（r=+0.97），而可解码性对此无效。因此，因果部署而非可解码性才是衡量模型是否使用知识的正确指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献普遍将线性探针能从模型激活中解码守恒量视为模型使用了该量的证据，作者质疑这一推断的合理性，旨在揭示可解码性与因果部署之间的根本性差距。

Method: 在机械、电路和PDE系统以及1.58亿参数的PDE基础模型上进行实验。使用单步激活交换（activation interchange）干预解码方向，测量对前向传播的影响（转移相关性τ）。比较在不同训练目标下守恒量的因果作用，并分析代数关系对部署的影响。

Result: 守恒量可达到R²≈1的线性可解码性但因果惰性（τ≈0）；改变训练目标使同一方向变为因果承载（τ→+1）；存在精确代数谓词控制部署；部署差距与OOD准确率高度相关（r=+0.97），而可解码性完全无关。

Conclusion: 可解码性不等于因果部署。解释性应测量因果部署来回答模型是否使用某知识的问题，并提供了廉价的测量工具（单步激活交换）。

Abstract: A linear probe that recovers a conserved quantity from a learned dynamics model's activations is routinely read as evidence that the model uses that quantity. We show this inference is unsound. Across mechanical, circuit, and partial-differential-equation (PDE) systems, and on a 158M-parameter pretrained PDE foundation model, energy and other invariants are linearly decodable at $R^2 \approx 1$ yet causally inert on next-state prediction: overwriting the decoded direction with a donor state's value (single-step activation interchange) leaves the forward pass essentially unchanged (transfer-corr $τ\approx 0$). The same direction in the same representation becomes causally load-bearing ($τ\to +1$) the moment the training objective rewards the invariant, so deployment is a property of the objective, not of the representation or the probe. We further show that when an invariant is deployed is governed by a precise algebraic predicate (its relation to the prediction output), by flipping a single invariant from inert to load-bearing by changing only the output's algebra. Finally, the gap has teeth: across models that all decode the target at $R^2 = 1.00$, the deployment gap forecasts out-of-distribution (OOD) accuracy ($r = +0.97$) where decodability is blind. We argue that causal deployment, not decodability, is what interpretability should measure when the question is whether a model uses a piece of knowledge, and we give a cheap instrument for measuring it.

</details>

### [49] [iFLYTEK-Embodied-Omni Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02542)
*Yuan Zhang,Jingfei Ni,Guanchen Lu,Shiqi Zhang,Qingshan Xu,Chi Liu,Xin Nie,Wenjie Xu,Lin Gao,Zhiyuan Cheng,Mingxin Zhou,Jiajia Wu,Diyuan Liu,Jia Pan,Chao Ji*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出iFLYTEK-Embodied-Omni，一个统一的多模态基础模型，通过共享多头自注意力机制联合建模视觉、语言和动作，采用"大脑-小脑"协作架构将高层语义理解与低层动作生成解耦，并通过四阶段训练策略在多种数据上实现端到端学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有具身智能方法通常将视觉-语言推理、视频世界建模和动作生成为级联流水线，导致接口瓶颈和复合预测误差。需要一种统一的框架来联合建模多种模态，避免信息丢失和错误累积。

Method: 提出iFLYTEK-Embodied-Omni统一多模态基础模型，包含视觉-语言模型(VLM)、视频生成模型(VGM)和动作生成模型(AGM)三个组件，通过共享多头自注意力机制通信。采用"大脑-小脑"架构：VLM和VGM作为高层大脑负责指令理解、任务规划、进度跟踪和未来状态预测；AGM作为低层小脑将子目标转换为可执行动作块。构建了包含人类演示和机器人交互的动作标注/无标注数据集的综合数据集，采用四阶段渐进训练策略。

Result: 该模型能够同时处理视觉、语言和动作模态，通过共享注意力机制实现跨模态协同，避免级联流水线中的接口瓶颈和误差累积问题，在长时程具身控制任务中实现精确的动作生成。

Conclusion: 统一多模态基础模型通过共享表示和"大脑-小脑"协作架构，能够有效解决具身智能中多模态理解、环境预测和动作生成的联合建模问题，为通用具身智能体提供了一种可行的技术路径。

Abstract: General-purpose embodied agents must understand multimodal instructions, anticipate how their environment will evolve, and produce precise control actions over extended horizons. Existing approaches typically specialize in visual-language reasoning, video-based world modeling, or action generation, while cascaded pipelines that first synthesize future observations and then infer actions can introduce interface bottlenecks and compound prediction errors. We present iFLYTEK-Embodied-Omni, a unified multimodal foundation model that jointly models vision(videos and images), language, and action within a single Omni framework. Its modality-specific visual-language, video-generation, and action-generation components communicate through shared multimodal self-attention. This design establishes brain-cerebellum collaboration: the vision-language modeland video generation model form a high-level brain for instruction understanding, task planning, progress tracking, and future visual-state prediction, whereas the action generation modelserves as a low-level cerebellum that directly converts planned subgoals and shared multimodal context into executable action chunks. To develop these capabilities, we combine action-annotated and action-free embodied videos from human demonstrations and robot interactions with embodied reasoning, embodied perception, and general-purpose image-text data to construct a comprehensive dataset. We further adopt a four-stage strategy that progressively trains the VLM, VGM, and AGM before jointly fine-tuning the complete model.

</details>


### [50] [ASK in the Dark: Uncertainty-Gated LLM Assistance under Partial Observability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02686)
*Juarez Monteiro,Nathan Gavenski,Guilherme Lima,Francisco Galuppo,Odinaldo Rodrigues,Adriano Veloso*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ASK+方法，通过向小语言模型提供轨迹感知上下文（部分地图、位置、动作历史）和结构化思维链推理，解决了部分可观测环境下SLM引导策略覆盖率为零的问题。在DoorKey、FourRooms、HigherLower等环境中显著提升性能，并证明提示设计与选择性门控比模型规模更重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在部分可观测环境中，强化学习智能体需基于不完整信息决策，小语言模型（SLM）具备广泛的推理先验，理论上适合提供引导。然而现有不确定性门控方法中SLM几乎从不贡献独立动作（覆盖率为零），问题根源在于提示过于自我中心化、缺乏上下文，而非SLM能力不足。

Method: 提出ASK+方法：1）向SLM提供轨迹感知上下文，包括部分揭示的地图、访问位置和动作历史；2）采用结构化思维链推理，将SLM从被动冗余检查转变为信息性顾问。同时验证预测熵信号测量的是动作不确定性而非状态不确定性，在POMDP中仍然有效。

Result: DoorKey上ASK+达93%（基线ASK与PPO均为89%）；FourRooms从53%提升至70%；HigherLower达73.7%，匹配SLM-only上限。Qwen3.5-2B与Qwen3.5-4B性能相当，证明提示设计主导效果。

Conclusion: 提示设计与选择性门控比模型规模更关键，无需大模型即可实现有效引导。预测熵在POMDP中仍可作为有效的查询信号。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning agents operating under partial observability must act on incomplete information, making them natural candidates for guidance from small language models (SLMs) that carry broad reasoning priors. Yet integrating SLM guidance into this setting has proven difficult: across all test environments, vanilla uncertainty-gated approaches achieve an overwrite rate at or near zero, meaning the SLM almost never contributes an independent action. We trace this failure to the bare egocentric prompt, which provides insufficient context for genuine reasoning, and identify it as a context problem rather than a capacity problem. We propose ASK+, which supplies the SLM with trajectory-aware context (a partially revealed map, visited positions, and action history) and structured chain-of-thought reasoning, converting it from a passive redundancy check into a more informative consultant that occasionally corrects the policy. We further establish that the predictive entropy signal used for selective querying measures action uncertainty rather than state uncertainty and remains informative in POMDPs, making uncertainty-gated assistance viable beyond fully observable settings. The stateful prompt drives substantial gains: on DoorKey, where vanilla ASK matches PPO (both 89%), ASK+ reaches 93% success; on FourRooms, success climbs from 53% to 70%; on HigherLower, accuracy reaches 73.7%, matching the SLM-only upper bound. Across all environments, Qwen3.5-2B matches or exceeds Qwen3.5-4B, confirming that prompt design and selective gating dominate the impact of model scale, enabling guidance without large models.

</details>


### [51] [SwarmResearch: Orchestrating Coding Agents for Open-Ended Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02807)
*Yuvraj Virk,Zack Edds,Chunqiu Steven Xia,Lingming Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SwarmResearch通过调度员-子智能体架构（Shepherd Agent + 多个Search Agent）解决长运行编码智能体容易收敛到单一高级方法的问题，在15项开放优化任务中13项达到或超越SOTA，通过高级探索和适应性并行搜索深度取得更好效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长运行编码智能体（如autoresearch）持续发现开放问题的优化方案，但倾向于收敛到单一高级方法后仅进行低级编辑，忽视了其他更优方法。作者认为两个设计选择导致该问题：在单个长运行智能体中积累上下文，以及只暴露单一程序状态供编辑。

Method: 提出SwarmResearch架构，包含一个Shepherd Agent（利用全局上下文引导）和一组Search Agent（每个在其各自的git分支中用局部上下文操作）。通过调度员引导的扩展，在不同搜索深度自适应调整并行度。

Result: 在开放优化任务上，SwarmResearch在15项任务中的13项上发现了比SOTA（LLM引导进化与多智能体技术）更好或相当的解。相比固定扩展的串行和并行智能体，调度员引导的扩展通过自适应并行度发现了更优解。

Conclusion: SwarmResearch通过解耦全局上下文与局部上下文，以及动态调整搜索并行度，有效缓解了长运行编码智能体的收敛性问题，在多样化开放优化任务上展现了更优的高级探索能力。

Abstract: Long-running coding agents such as autoresearch can persistently discover optimizations for open-ended problems. However, they tend to converge onto a single high-level approach, then proceed with low-level edits while missing other superior approaches to the problem. We hypothesize two harness-level design choices contribute to this behavior: accumulating context in a single long-running agent and only exposing a single program state to edit. We introduce SwarmResearch, an orchestrator-subagent harness in which a Shepherd Agent uses global context to steer a population of Search Agents, each operating with local context in their respective git branch. On open-ended optimization tasks, SwarmResearch discovers better or comparable solutions to state-of-the-art LLM-guided evolution and multi-agent techniques on 13/15 tasks, driven by higher-level exploration. Compared with fixed scaling of serial and parallel agents, SwarmResearch's orchestrator-guided scaling discovers better-performing solutions by adapting parallelism at different search depths.

</details>


### [52] [Object-Centric Environment Modeling for Agentic Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02846)
*Yiyang Li,Tianyi Ma,Zehong Wang,Yijun Ma,Yanfang Ye*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出对象中心环境建模（OCM），将智能体交互经验组织为可执行的对象中心环境模型（包含对象知识与过程知识两个代码库），通过在线学习、反射更新和验证机制，提升LLM智能体性能并减少无效动作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体可通过经验积累提升性能，但自由形式的文本记忆随着交互增长难以维护、验证和复用；现有的符号化方法存储局部过程或假设简化动态，缺乏对环境的系统性、可执行的结构化建模。

Method: OCM维护两个相连的代码库：对象知识（用Python类定义环境实体与机制）和过程知识（记录可复用的交互模式）。在线设置下，每个episode结束后反射轨迹、更新两个知识库，并验证所有过程在更新后的对象模型上可执行。未来交互中使用渐进知识披露，先检查紧凑代码签名，仅在需要时阅读源码。

Result: 实验表明OCM在多个基准上取得最佳平均排名，并减少了无效动作。

Conclusion: 智能体可以通过构建对象中心的环境模型来获得性能提升。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents can improve through accumulated experience, but free-form textual memories become difficult to maintain, validate, and reuse as interactions grow. Recent symbolic approaches learn executable skills or programmatic world models, yet often store local procedures or assume simplified dynamics. We propose Object-Centric Environment Modeling (OCM), which organizes experience into an executable object-centric environment model. OCM maintains two connected code bases: object knowledge, which defines environment entities and mechanisms as Python classes, and procedure knowledge, which records reusable interaction patterns that must import and use the object model. OCM works in an online setting: after each episode, OCM reflects on the trajectory, updates both knowledge bases, and verifies that all procedures execute against the updated object model. During future interaction, the agent uses progressive knowledge disclosure to inspect compact code signatures first and read source code only when needed. Experiments show that OCM achieves the best average rank across benchmarks and reduces invalid actions, demonstrating that agents can benefit from building object-centric environment models.

</details>


### [53] [MedCalc-Pro: Solving Complex Medical Calculations with LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02879)
*Siran Zhao,Ruihui Hou,Ziyue Huai,Chennuo Zhang,Tong Ruan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出医疗计算基准MedCalc-Pro，涵盖单计算器、多计算器和嵌套计算器三种渐进式任务场景，包含2,268个真实临床病例和77个医疗计算器；同时提出支持多工具选择和嵌套工具调用的通用智能体框架，通过结构化验证和证据审查抑制参数错误传播，实验表明该框架在所有任务设置中均取得最佳性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医疗计算基准过于简化（单计算器、明确指定工具），无法反映真实临床中需要多计算器联合评估、嵌套尺度计算及模糊查询的复杂场景，亟需更贴合实际的基准与方法来评估大语言模型在复杂医疗计算中的能力。

Method: 构建MedCalc-Pro基准（2,268例真实病例，77个计算器，14个科室，三种任务设置）；提出通用智能体框架，支持多工具选择和嵌套工具调用，并通过结构化验证与证据审查机制抑制参数错误传播。

Result: 在开源、闭源和医学专用大语言模型上的系统对比显示，所提框架在所有三个任务设置中均取得最佳性能。

Conclusion: MedCalc-Pro为评估大语言模型在复杂医疗计算场景中的能力提供了更具挑战性的基准，所提出的智能体框架有效提升了多工具和嵌套工具场景下的表现。

Abstract: Current benchmarks for evaluating large language models (LLMs) in medical calculation are largely based on simplified settings, where each patient case corresponds to a single calculator and the required tool is explicitly specified in the query. However, real clinical scenarios often require multiple calculators for joint evaluation, nested-scale calculation, and fuzzy queries that do not directly specify the target calculator. To this end, we propose a new medical calculation benchmark, MedCalc-Pro, which covers three progressively challenging task settings: single-calculator, multi-calculator, and nested-calculator calculation settings. MedCalc-Pro contains 2,268 real-world clinical cases, covering 77 medical calculators across 14 clinical departments. Meanwhile, to address the limited performance of existing frameworks and methods in complex clinical scenarios, we further propose a more generalizable agent framework that supports multi-tool selection and nested-tool calling, while suppressing parameter error propagation through structured validation and evidence review. We conduct systematic comparisons across open-source, closed-source, and medical-specialized LLMs, and the results show that our framework achieves the best performance across all three task settings. This work provides a new benchmark and method for evaluating and applying LLMs in challenging medical calculation scenarios.

</details>


### [54] [Folding, Reasoning, and Scaling with Open-source Drug Discovery Engine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03787)
*Aureka AI OpenDDE project*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: OpenDDE是一个开源的全原子生物分子基础模型，以共折叠为切入点构建可扩展的AI驱动药物发现引擎，在达到IsoDDE级共折叠精度的同时，提供结构预测、从头设计、亲和力评估等能力，并揭示了共折叠模型的两个缩放定律方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确建模生物分子相互作用是生物学和治疗发现的核心瓶颈，现有方法往往将结构预测视为孤立终点，缺乏统一的结构推理框架。

Method: 采用全原子架构、原子潜在推理、推理优化和大规模数据处理等多项技术，以共折叠作为共享结构推理层，构建统一框架用于建模序列-结构-功能关系。

Result: 实现了IsoDDE级别的共折叠精度，并识别出共折叠模型的两个缩放定律方向（数据、模型、推理和训练缩放），提供了可复现的开源框架。

Conclusion: OpenDDE旨在民主化前沿生物分子智能的访问，加速全球协作，为下一代药物发现系统（从预测分子结构到设计、评分和优化治疗候选物）奠定开放基础。

Abstract: Accurately modeling biomolecular interactions is a central bottleneck in biology and therapeutic discovery. Here, we introduce Open Drug Discovery Engine (OpenDDE), an open-source, all-atom biomolecular foundation model that uses co-folding as the entry point to a scalable AI-driven drug discovery engine. Rather than treating structure prediction as an isolated endpoint, OpenDDE is designed as a shared structural reasoning layer for modeling sequence-structure-function relationships across biomolecular complexes, enabling complex structure prediction today while providing a foundation for de novo design, affinity estimation, structure-conditioned optimization, and more. OpenDDE integrates advances in all-atom architecture, atomic latent reasoning, inference optimization, and large-scale data processing to achieve IsoDDE-level co-folding accuracy within a reproducible and openly accessible framework. We also identify two scaling-law directions for co-folding models, revealing practical routes for continued improvement through data, model, inference, and training scaling. By releasing training code, inference pipelines, checkpoints, and benchmarks, OpenDDE aims to democratize access to frontier biomolecular intelligence, accelerate global collaboration, and lay an open foundation for next-generation drug discovery systems that can move from predicting molecular structures toward designing, scoring, and optimizing therapeutic candidates for human health.

</details>


### [55] [Silicon Sampling via Cross-Survey Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03091)
*Chan-Tung Ku,Chan Hsu,Pei-Cing Huang,Frank Cheng-shan Liu,I-Ling Cheng,Yihuang Kang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出交叉调查迁移框架评估LLM模拟人类受访者的能力，发现零样本LLM在个体层面预测准确率达52%，仅比监督式随机森林低6个百分点；不同构念的可预测性存在显著差异（从政党态度的67%到主权问题的23%）；方差塌缩和安全对齐效应比以往报告更为复杂。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对硅采样（用LLM模拟人类调查受访者）的评估大多停留在分布层面的比较，而非个体层面的预测，这种做法容易将模式匹配与一致的个体层面预测混为一谈。作者因此提出更严格的评估框架。

Method: 提出交叉调查迁移（cross-survey transfer）框架：给LLM同一调查中一组问题的回答，要求其预测该受访者对完全不同问题的答案。使用TEDS 2024数据、三个开源LLM（27B-120B参数）和监督式机器学习基线进行对比。

Result: (1)零样本LLM在真正未见项目上达到52%准确率，仅比监督式随机森林低6个百分点；(2)不同构念可预测性存在稳定层级（政党态度67%→主权问题23%）；(3)方差塌缩和安全性对齐效应比以往报告更复杂。

Conclusion: 这些发现厘清了硅采样的潜力与边界，表明LLM在个体层面预测上具有可观能力，但存在构念间的显著差异，且常见的LLM局限性需要更细致的理解。

Abstract: Silicon sampling-using large language models (LLMs) to simulate human survey respondents-has emerged as a promising approach for augmenting traditional survey research. However, most evaluations rely on distributional comparisons rather than individual-level prediction, which risks conflating pattern matching with coherent respondent-level prediction. We propose cross-survey transfer, a more rigorous evaluation framework in which an LLM is given a respondent's answers to one set of questions and must predict their answers to entirely different questions from the same survey. Using data from the Taiwan Election and Democratization Study (TEDS) 2024, three open-weight LLMs (27B-120B parameters), and supervised machine learning baselines, we find that: (1) zero-shot LLMs achieve 52% accuracy on genuinely unseen items, closing to within 6 percentage points (pp) of a supervised random forest trained on same-population data; (2) a stable construct predictability hierarchy emerges, from 67% for partisan attitudes to 23% for sovereignty; and (3) variance collapse and safety alignment effects-two commonly cited LLM limitations-turn out to be more nuanced than previously reported, with variance collapse affecting supervised models as well and alignment effects varying dramatically across model families. These findings clarify both the promise and boundaries of silicon sampling.

</details>


### [56] [APeB: Benchmarking Personalization Ability of Large Language Model Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03162)
*Garry Yang,Zizhe Chen,Xinru Chen,Yongqiang Chen,Jianxiang Wang,Deyu Zou,Linyi Ding,Jialiang Wu,Yunzhong He,Yu Gong,James Cheng,Huaixiao Tou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出个性化产品搜索（PPS）基准和Agent个性化基准（APeB），用于评估LLM智能体在原始、未明确指定查询下的个性化推荐能力。研究发现现有模型擅长处理显式查询，但在需要意图推断和偏好发现的早期阶段查询中表现不佳，主要原因是历史利用不充分。提出的VQRA（历史感知查询精炼流水线）能有效提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试大多依赖用户精炼后的查询或简化历史记录，无法有效评估LLM智能体在原始、未充分指定的用户查询下进行个性化推荐的能力。

Method: 构建个性化产品搜索（PPS）测试平台，从行为日志中构建APeB基准，配对未明确指定的意图与丰富的交互历史及用户浏览过的候选物品。采用多步智能体工作流评估SOTA LLM，并提出VQRA（历史感知查询精炼流水线）。

Result: 模型能很好处理显式查询，但在需要意图推断和偏好发现的早期查询上表现不佳；归因分析表明主要原因是历史信息利用不充分；VQRA可带来一致的性能提升。

Conclusion: 个性化智能体需要专门的历史利用模块来提升在原始查询下的个性化推荐能力。

Abstract: LLM-powered agents struggle with personalization when users issue raw, underspecified queries. In this setting, agents must infer latent intent, extract preferences from noisy interaction histories, and select among competing alternatives. Existing benchmarks rarely test this capability, as they often rely on user-refined queries or simplified histories. We introduce personalized product search (PPS), a testbed for agentic personalization under raw queries and diverse histories. We construct Agent Personalized Benchmark (APeB) from action logs, pairing underspecified intents with rich histories and user-viewed candidate items. Evaluating state-of-the-art LLMs with multi-step agent workflows, we find that models handle explicit queries well but struggle with early-stage queries requiring intent and preference discovery. Rubric analysis attributes this gap mainly to ineffective history use. A simple history-aware query-refinement pipeline, VQRA, yields consistent gains, highlighting the need for dedicated history-utilization modules in personalized agents.

</details>


### [57] [Organizational Memory for Agentic Business Process Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03228)
*Lukas Kirchdorfer,Adrian Rebmann,Christian Warmuth,Timotheus Kampik,Theiss Heilker,Gregor Berg*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种组织记忆架构，用于解决基于LLM的智能体在企业业务流程执行中面临的知识碎片化、知识孤岛和规则重复问题，通过构建共享、可治理、智能体可消费的参考层来存储组织特定的程序性知识，并在采购场景的概念验证中展示了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用LLM缺乏组织特定的知识，现有方法（如将知识编码到单个提示或智能体专属检索设置）在企业中难以扩展，会导致知识孤岛、规则重复，且难以进行一致更新和跨智能体学习。

Method: 推导组织记忆的需求，提出一个用于知识策展和消费的架构，该架构作为共享、可治理、智能体可消费的参考层，存储演化的组织特定程序性知识。

Result: 在采购场景的概念验证中展示了所提架构的有效性。

Conclusion: 组织记忆是实现企业级智能体业务流程执行的关键组件，能有效解决知识管理问题，支持可扩展的自动化执行。

Abstract: LLM-based agents offer new opportunities for automating business process execution beyond the limits of rule-based systems. However, general-purpose LLMs lack the organization-specific knowledge required for reliable execution, which is typically fragmented across human-oriented artifacts such as policies, process models, and standard operating procedures. While such knowledge can technically be encoded in individual prompts or agent-specific retrieval setups, this approach does not scale in enterprises, as it gives rise to knowledge silos and rule duplicates, and makes consistent updates and learning across agents difficult. We argue that this calls for an organizational memory for agentic business process execution: a shared, governed, and agent-consumable reference layer of evolving organization-specific procedural knowledge about how work should be executed. We derive requirements for such a memory, propose an architecture for its curation and consumption, and demonstrate its effectiveness in a proof-of-concept based on a procurement scenario.

</details>


### [58] [Reflective Dialogue or Prompt Refinement? Effects of Tutor Scaffolding on Students' Independent LLM Use for Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03303)
*Jerome Brender,Laila El-Hamamsy,Kim Uittenhove,Aitor Perez,Patrick Jermann,Francesco Mondada,Engin Bumbacher*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究对比了两种LLM辅导机器人（苏格拉底引导式SG vs 提示精炼式PR）在研究生机器人课程中的效果，发现SG辅导器虽被认为效率较低，但能带来更高的学习收益，并促使学生在后续无约束使用LLM时采用理解驱动型提示策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在教育中的应用日益广泛，但学习效果取决于学生如何与LLM互动。现有研究对LLM在教育中的使用存在担忧，缺乏对不同LLM辅导器设计如何影响学生提示实践、学习效果及后续LLM使用行为的系统比较。

Method: 两阶段研究设计：第一阶段（6周干预），66名研究生分别使用SG辅导器（通过对话式提问结构化交互）或PR辅导器（指导有效提示 formulation）；第二阶段（3周课程项目），52名学生使用无约束LLM。在研究生级别的移动机器人课程中进行实验。

Result: SG和PR辅导器在引导使用期间的任务表现和提示模式相似，但学习效果和后续LLM使用存在差异：SG学生相比PR学生获得了更高的学习收益，在无约束LLM使用时更倾向于采用理解驱动型提示策略（该策略可预测更高理解水平）。

Conclusion: 尽管学习者认为SG辅导器效率较低，但苏格拉底式引导能长期支持学生发展使用LLM学习的能力，这对LLM辅导器的设计具有重要意义。

Abstract: While Large Language Models (LLMs) can provide personalized support in learning, several studies have raised concerns regarding their use in education. Importantly, learning depends on how students engage with LLMs. This study examined how two types of LLM-based tutors shape students' prompting practices, learning, and subsequent LLM-use: a Socratic-Guidance (SG) tutor, which structures interaction through dialogic questioning, and a Prompt-Refinement (PR) tutor that guides the formulation of effective prompts. We conducted a two-phase study in a graduate-level mobile robotics course: 66 students used either the SG or PR tutor during a 6-week intervention, followed by 52 students using an unconstrained LLM during a 3-week course project. Results show that while the SG- and PR tutors led to similar task performance and prompting patterns during guided use, they differ in learning outcomes and later LLM-use. SG-students, relative to PR-student, achieved higher learning gains in later sessions, and were more likely to adopt understanding-driven prompting strategies, which are predictive of higher understanding, when using an unconstrained LLM. Although learners perceived the SG tutor as less efficient, the findings suggest that Socratic guidance supports the development of students' capacity to learn with LLMs over time, highlighting its importance for LLM tutor design.

</details>


### [59] [When Aggregate Alignment Misleads: Auditing Policy Repair Without Per-State Expert Actions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03386)
*Peiying Zhu,Sidi Chang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究在缺乏逐状态专家标签的情况下，利用仅有区域级诊断反馈（价格分布摘要）的智能体策略编辑器（多重启LLM）进行酒店定价策略修复。实验表明，LLM编辑器能达到接近基准策略的RevPAR（108.47 vs 108.75），并显著降低情节组成距离（从1.153降至0.609），但非语义提议器或打乱诊断格式均无法达到相同效果。树编辑器虽对齐更优但收入更低（98.91）。结论指出，智能体策略修复应基于诊断反馈是否成为可靠闭环结果来评估，而非单一行为距离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在缺乏逐状态专家动作标签的现实场景中，评估智能体AI对决策策略的编辑、优化和修复效果非常困难。现有方法通常需要细粒度标签或奖励信号，但实际系统中往往只能获得区域级汇总反馈。

Method: 使用酒店定价模拟器，多重启LLM作为策略编辑器，仅基于区域级诊断反馈（价格分布对比摘要）来编辑目标动作表。编辑器无法访问基准动作、源代码、奖励数值或保留结果，只能提出受限编辑。设置打乱诊断、非语义提议器等多种对照实验。

Result: LLM编辑器RevPAR达到108.47（接近基准108.75），与基准差距-0.276（95% CI [-0.692, 0.146]），情节组成距离从1.153降至0.609。打乱诊断对照组RevPAR降至94.30，非语义提议器落后8.77-14.57。树编辑器对齐更优但收入仅98.91。

Conclusion: 智能体策略修复不应仅用单一行为距离指标评估，而应考察诊断反馈能否转化为可靠的闭环结果。最优修复不一定是最佳模仿，需综合结果与过程度量。

Abstract: Agentic AI systems are increasingly used to edit, refine, and repair decision policies, but evaluating these edits is difficult when per-state expert action labels are unavailable. We study this problem in a hotel-pricing simulator where an agentic policy editor receives only region-level diagnostic feedback: summaries of how its price distribution differs from a benchmark policy across time, inventory, and market regions. The editor cannot observe benchmark actions, benchmark source code, reward numbers, or held-out outcomes, and may only propose constrained edits to a target-action table. On 5,000 held-out episodes, a multi-restart LLM editor reaches RevPAR 108.47 (95% CI 107.61 - 109.34), close to the benchmark policy's 108.75 (107.81 - 109.68), with paired gap (LLM minus benchmark) -0.276 and 95% CI [-0.692, 0.146]. A cheap diagnostic projection already recovers much of the revenue (107.90), so the LLM editor's distinctive gain is not raw revenue lift alone: it also reduces episode composition distance from 1.153 to 0.609. This is the strongest non-benchmark repair result. This profile is not explained by restart search alone: non-semantic proposers with up to 2,500 evaluations fall 8.77 - 14.57 RevPAR points short. Nor is it explained by plausible prompt format: a shuffled-diagnostic control breaks region-error correspondence and falls to RevPAR 94.30. The match is genuine but partial. A tree editor achieves stronger pooled alignment, 0.214 versus 0.266, and stronger reference-state D1, 0.328 versus 1.197, yet revenue falls to 98.91. These results show that agentic policy repair should be evaluated by whether diagnostic feedback becomes reliable closed-loop outcome, not by a single behavioral distance.

</details>


### [60] [From Mobile Data to Business Insights: An End-to-End Analytics Framework for Large-Scale Urban Mobility Analysis and Decision Support](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03394)
*Thiago Andrade,Shazia Tabassum,Miguel E. P. Silva,Ricardo Dinis,Joao Gama*

  <summary>Details</summary>
